Result: 训练后的模型能够成功重构完整的I-V曲线，并直接提取关键器件指标，包括阈值电压（VTH）、亚阈值斜率（SS）和峰值跨导（gm）；在极少量训练数据下即可实现高精度建模。

Conclusion: 基于实际表征数据构建的数据驱动紧凑模型能够以高精度和低数据需求实现器件建模，为快速器件表征、建模和电路级仿真提供了强有力的工具。

Abstract: This work presents a machine learning framework that leverages an autoencoder (AE) for the efficient modeling of FinFET. We first calibrated a BSIM-CMG model to generate a dataset of current-voltage (ID-VG) characteristics. This data was used to train an autoencoder that compresses full I-V curves into a low-dimensional latent space, which intrinsically encodes key device physics. A key innovation is the explicit incorporation of parameter such as drain to source voltage (VDS) as an input feature, enhancing the model ability to capture bias dependent variation. The trained model successfully reconstructs full I-V curves and directly extracts critical device metrics including threshold voltage (VTH), subthreshold slope (SS), and peak transconductance (gm). This approach demonstrates that data driven compact models, built from actual characterization data, can achieve high accuracy with minimal training data, providing a powerful tool for rapid device characterization, modelling and circuit level simulation.

</details>


### [36] [RAVEN: A Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network for Financial Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24062)
*Cheng He,Zhenyu Guan,Xijie Liang,Defu Lian,Jiajia Li,Enhong Chen,Patrick P. C. Lee,Geng Hu,Zehao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAVEN框架，通过自适应确定时间上下文长度来解决金融时间序列预测中固定窗口不匹配问题，在多个金融数据集上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列预测面临非平稳性、极低信噪比和依赖机制变化的挑战。现有SOTA模型使用固定上下文窗口，与非平稳价格过程的时变最优回溯窗口不匹配，这是一个关键局限性。

Method: 提出Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network (RAVEN)混合专家框架：(1)通过累积重要性阈值(CIT)机制构建嵌套连续窗口层次，自适应确定每个样本的时间上下文；(2)设计全局压缩表示(GCR)分支保持全局时间一致性；(3)引入相关性感知加权(CAW)对齐变长专家输出并对聚合前的成对余弦相似度进行惩罚。

Result: 在HS300上Pearson相关性提升9.2%，在S&P500上提升20.2%；在基金销售预测上MSE降低18.2%；在四个PEMS交通基准的16个指标中取得14个最佳结果，实现SOTA性能。

Conclusion: RAVEN通过自适应变量上下文机制有效解决了金融时间序列的非平稳性问题，显著优于固定窗口的现有方法，证明了机制感知的变长建模在时间序列预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Financial time series forecasting presents structural challenges absent from standard benchmarks. Log-returns are non-stationary, exhibit exceptionally low signal-to-noise (SNR) ratios, and are governed by regime-dependent temporal dependencies. We identify a key limitation of state-of-the-art (SOTA) time series models in financial settings. A fixed context window is mismatched to the time-varying optimal look-back of non-stationary price processes. We propose the Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network (RAVEN), a Mixture-of-Experts framework designed to adaptively determine the temporal context for each input sample. Instead of relying on a fixed look-back horizon, RAVEN constructs a hierarchy of nested contiguous windows whose lengths are determined by the data itself. Specifically, RAVEN scores patches by learned importance in reverse chronological order and applies the Cumulative Importance Thresholding (CIT) mechanism to derive nested prefix windows, each routed to a scale-specialized expert. A Global Compressed Representation (GCR) branch runs in parallel over the full context, preserving global temporal coherence that local experts cannot guarantee. Because the nested routing induces structured overlap among expert inputs, we introduce a Correlation-Aware Weighting (CAW) to align variable-length expert outputs and penalize pairwise cosine similarity prior to aggregation. Experiments on cumulative log-return prediction (HS300, S&P500) and fund sales forecasting demonstrate that RAVEN achieves SOTA performances, improves Pearson correlation by 9.2% on HS300 and 20.2% on S&P500, and reduces MSE by 18.2% on fund sales forecasting, while achieving the best results in 14 of 16 metrics on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [37] [Blockwise Policy-Drift Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24084)
*Liwen Zheng,Haiyun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出块级策略漂移门控方法，通过控制新旧学生策略间的漂移来改进在线策略蒸馏（OPD），在Qwen3数学推理基准上平均pass@8从0.4978提升至0.5160。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，采样token的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在长序列推理任务上表现脆弱，局部教师支持匹配虽然是一种有效的修复方法，但仍需更好的漂移控制机制来提升rollout重用场景下的训练稳定性和效果。

Method: 提出块级策略漂移门控（blockwise policy-drift gating）方法：(1)计算行为学生策略与当前学生策略在采样token路径上的对数概率偏移；(2)在固定块或跨度上聚合这些偏移；(3)使用去均值的归一化门控值重新加权OPD位置损失。该方法仅修改学生策略，不改变教师目标、教师Top-K支持或rollout策略。

Result: 在六种变体的Qwen3数学推理基准测试中（统一200步训练预算，以pass@8为主要评估指标）：固定64 token块门控将采样token OPD的平均pass@8从0.4978提升至0.5160（在AIME24、AIME25、MATH500和AMC23四个基准上）；在Teacher-TopK/LSM设置下，Block64在所有训练学生模型中取得最佳的四基准平均pass@8。

Conclusion: 局部新旧策略漂移可作为重用OPD rollouts的实用控制信号，块级门控是一种简单有效的默认方法，能够提升求解率的鲁棒性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student policy using teacher signals computed on trajectories sampled by the student itself. Recent work shows that sampled-token OPD can be fragile on long-horizon reasoning tasks and that local teacher-support matching is a simple and effective repair. This paper introduces blockwise policy-drift gating, a lightweight student-only old-current drift controller for OPD under rollout reuse. The method computes log-probability shifts between the behavior student and the current student on the sampled token path, aggregates these shifts over fixed blocks or spans, and uses the resulting detached, mean-normalized gates to reweight OPD position losses. It does not change teacher targets, teacher top-K supports, or the rollout policy. In a six-variant Qwen3 math reasoning benchmark with a uniform 200-step training budget for all trained variants, we use pass@8 as the primary problem-level solve-rate metric. Fixed 64-token block gating improves sampled-token OPD mean pass@8 from 0.4978 to 0.5160 across AIME24, AIME25, MATH500, and AMC23. On Teacher-TopK/LSM, Block64 gives the best four-benchmark mean pass@8 among trained students. The results identify local old-current policy drift as a practical control signal for reused OPD rollouts and motivate block-level gating as a simple default for improving solve-rate robustness.

</details>

*Wenhao Gao,Yifan Wang,Yijia Ma,Carl Yang,Wen Li,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeuroSonic，一种基于条件流匹配的EEG到语音重建框架，通过确定性概率流速度场在EEG条件下将噪声音频状态传输到清晰语音，避免了随机采样的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从头皮脑电图(EEG)重建连续语音面临根本性挑战：EEG信号微弱、空间扩散且高度可变，而语音具有强谐波和时间结构，这种不匹配导致波形回归不稳定，随机多步生成对伪影相关条件和被试者变异性敏感。

Method: 提出NeuroSonic框架，采用条件流匹配方法学习确定性概率流速度场，在EEG条件下将噪声损坏的声学状态传输到清晰语音；将EEG和音频嵌入共享标记空间，通过时间条件门控Transformer参数化传输常微分方程，显式建模轨迹演化并避免迭代随机采样。

Result: 在CineBrain和EAV基准数据集的跨被试者评估中，相比GAN、扩散和平均流基线方法，NeuroSonic在分布真实感、频谱保真度和感知质量方面均有提升，总体感知质量提升高达26.3%，在伪影严重的片段中性能差距最为明显。

Conclusion: 确定性条件传输为EEG驱动的语音重建提供了稳定有效的解决方案，特别是在处理伪影重、条件变异性强的片段时表现出明显优势。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous speech from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) remains fundamentally challenging. EEG provides a weak, spatially diffuse, and highly variable measurement of distributed cortical activity, whereas speech is organized as a coherent acoustic trajectory with strong harmonic and temporal structure. The resulting mismatch makes waveform regression unstable and causes stochastic multi-step generation to be sensitive to artifact-dependent conditioning and subject variability. We introduce NeuroSonic, a conditional flow-matching framework for EEG-to-speech reconstruction. Instead of predicting waveforms directly or refining them through stochastic denoising, NeuroSonic learns a deterministic probability-flow velocity field that transports a noise-corrupted acoustic state toward clean speech under EEG conditioning. EEG and audio are embedded into a shared token space and processed by a time-conditioned gated Transformer that parameterizes the transport ordinary differential equation. This formulation models trajectory evolution explicitly while avoiding iterative stochastic sampling. We evaluate NeuroSonic on the CineBrain and EAV benchmarks under cross-subject evaluation. Across both datasets, the proposed method improves distributional realism, spectral fidelity, and perceptual quality over representative GAN-, diffusion-, and mean-flow baselines, with up to a 26.3\% gain in overall perceptual quality. The performance gap is most evident in artifact-heavy segments, where conditioning variability is strongest. These findings indicate that deterministic conditional transport provides a stable and effective formulation for EEG-driven speech reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/Y-Research-SBU/NeuroSonic/ .

</details>


### [39] [FedUP: One-Shot Federated Unlearning via Centroid-Guided Plug-in Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24113)
*Feihong Nan,Zhengyi Zhong,Pan Wang,Weidong Bao,Xiongtao Zhang,Quan Wen,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedUP，一种一次性联邦遗忘框架，通过在服务器端训练轻量级可插拔过滤器作为“知识漏斗”，在几秒钟内精准遗忘目标数据，有效解决了非目标知识丢失与高延迟的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦遗忘方法面临非目标知识丢失与高请求延迟之间的持续困境，亟需一种既能满足“被遗忘权”法律合规要求，又能保证模型性能与效率的解决方案。

Method: 提出FedUP框架，核心是冻结原始模型参数，在服务器端利用受差分隐私（DP）保护的类中心样本训练轻量级可插拔过滤器。该过滤器充当“知识漏斗”筛选目标数据，无需多轮客户端-服务器通信或复杂再训练，且可通过移除过滤器无缝恢复被遗忘知识。

Result: 在多种图像和文本任务上的广泛实验表明，FedUP有效减少了非目标知识丢失，在各种场景下实现了卓越的遗忘精度与效率，将遗忘延迟从分钟级缩短至秒级。

Conclusion: FedUP通过一次性服务器端过滤器训练，成功解决了联邦遗忘中的效率与精度平衡难题，实现了快速、精准且可逆的知识遗忘，为去中心化系统的合规性提供了有效技术手段。

Abstract: Federated unlearning (FU) is critical for complying with legal mandates like the right to be forgotten in decentralized systems, yet current methods face a persistent dilemma between non-target knowledge loss and high request latency. To resolve these issues, we propose FedUP, a one-shot federated unlearning framework utilizing lightweight pluggable filters that act as a "knowledge funnel" to screen out target data while preserving original model performance. By freezing original model parameters and training filters at the server side using differentially private (DP)-protected class centroid samples, FedUP bypasses the need for multi-round client-server communication and complex retraining, reducing unlearning latency from minutes to mere seconds. Additionally, the framework's pluggable architecture ensures inherent reversibility, enabling the seamless restoration of forgotten knowledge by simply removing the filters. Extensive experiments on diverse image and text tasks demonstrate that FedUP effectively reduces non-target knowledge loss and achieves superior unlearning precision and efficiency across various scenarios. Code is available at: https://github.com/suows/FedUP-code.

</details>

*Lucky Verma,Pratik Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在816个LoRA/PEFT配置中，top-1 argmax集中度作为崩溃预警信号精确度为零，因其在优化前已饱和；而最大LoRA梯度范数在LLaDA家族上实现了0.68的精确度和0.79的F1值，能有效识别最终损失最低的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型（DLM）微调继承了去噪时间置信度监控器的低成本诊断方法，但这些信号在PEFT训练中的有效性未经测试。研究旨在验证top-1 argmax集中度作为崩溃预警信号的可靠性，并寻找更有效的替代指标。

Method: 在三个DLM家族的816个LoRA/PEFT配置上测试top-1 argmax集中度作为崩溃预警信号；分析其失效原因为预平衡饱和现象；评估最大LoRA梯度范数作为替代指标，该指标通过采样梯度路由而非token集中度；在LLaDA家族的保留验证集上优化阈值并验证性能。

Result: top-1 argmax集中度预警在所有816个配置中均触发，但实际零崩溃发生，精确度为零，原因是优化前已高度集中且对最终训练稳定性不敏感；最大LoRA梯度范数在识别最终损失最低的top-decile配置时达到精确度0.68和F1=0.79，优于全正基线，但结果限于短视野DLM-LoRA检查而非通用崩溃检测器。

Conclusion: 建议放弃top-1 argmax集中度作为PEFT预警信号，改为在训练早期记录最大梯度范数，并在每个DLM家族上校准阈值后再进行路由检查；自回归控制和跨家族阈值失败表明该方法不具普适性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language model (DLM) fine-tuning inherits inexpensive diagnostics from denoising-time confidence monitors, but their PEFT-training meaning is untested. We test top-1 argmax concentration as a collapse warning. Across 816 LoRA/PEFT configurations from three DLM families, the warning fires for every configuration while logs record 0/816 actual collapses at the 200 step horizon, giving zero precision. The cause is pre-equilibrium saturation: top-1 concentration is already high before optimization and quickly becomes insensitive to final training stability. We then evaluate max LoRA gradient norm, a parameter-side signal that samples gradient routing rather than token concentration. On a pooled held-out LLaDA-family split, a train-optimized threshold identifies top-decile final-loss configurations with precision 0.68 and F1=0.79, above the all-positive top-1 baseline even at the lower split-bootstrap confidence bound. Autoregressive controls and cross-family threshold failures bound the result to short-horizon DLM-LoRA inspection rather than a universal collapse detector. Workflow: drop top-1 as a PEFT alarm, log max-gradient early in training, and calibrate thresholds per DLM family before routing runs for inspection.

</details>


### [41] [Holistic Data Scheduler for LLM Pre-training via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24133)
*Chenhao Dang,Jing Ma,Mingjie Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的在线数据混合框架HDS，通过多目标奖励函数从数据质量、域间影响和模型参数三个维度动态调整训练数据配比，在减少44%训练迭代次数的同时提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线数据混合（ODM）方法仅依赖单一优化视角，无法满足大语言模型预训练中从多个维度动态考虑数据组合的需求。LLM预训练需要综合考虑数据质量、域间相互影响和模型状态等多个方面来动态调整训练数据配比。

Method: 提出Holistic Data Scheduler（HDS）框架，将数据调度问题建模为连续控制空间中的强化学习问题。采用Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）算法以在高维策略空间中实现稳定且样本高效的探索。设计了多目标综合奖励函数，整合三个关键视角：基于数据质量的奖励、基于损失捕获域间影响的奖励、以及基于权重范数的模型驱动奖励。

Result: 在The Pile基准测试中，HDS以减少44%的训练迭代次数达到次优方法的最终验证困惑度。在MMLU 0-shot任务上实现7.2%的性能提升，并在其他基准测试中均取得一致的性能增益。

Conclusion: HDS成功解决了现有ODM方法的局限性，通过多维度综合奖励函数实现了训练效率和最终模型能力的双重提升，为大语言模型的预训练数据策略提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: The composition of training data, governed by the diversity of sources and their mixing strategy, is a cornerstone of Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training. Online Data Mixing (ODM), the technique of adaptively adjusting data mixtures during training, has emerged as a promising direction to improve efficiency. However, existing methods are constrained by their reliance on a singular optimization perspective, which fundamentally overlooks the need for complex LLM pre-training to consider the dynamic data composition from multiple dimensions. To overcome this limitation, we introduce the Holistic Data Scheduler (HDS), a novel online data mixing framework. HDS formulates the data scheduling challenge as a reinforcement learning problem in a continuous control space and leverages the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm for its stability and sample efficiency in exploring the high-dimensional policy space. At the core of HDS lies a novel multi-objective, holistic reward function that integrates three critical perspectives: a data-driven reward for quality, a loss-driven reward capturing inter-domain influence, and a model-driven reward based on weight norms. To validate our design and determine its optimal configuration, we conducted systematic experiments on LLMs of various sizes. On The Pile benchmark, HDS reaches the final validation perplexity of the next best method with 44% fewer training iterations. Furthermore, it achieves a 7.2% improvement on the MMLU 0-shot task along with consistent gains on other benchmarks, showcasing its ability to enhance both training efficiency and final model capability.

</details>


### [42] [A Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation Sampler for Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24140)
*Feiyang Fu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TR-CIE采样器，通过时间重参数化和累积强度外推，在有限函数评估次数（NFE）下显著提升了离散流匹配（DFM）的采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流匹配（DFM）是离散状态空间生成建模的有力框架，但在函数评估次数（NFE）受限时，现有采样方法（如τ-leaping）的采样质量不足，缺乏高效的采样策略。

Method: 提出TR-CIE采样器，包含两个核心组件：（1）基于调度的时间重参数化，根据噪声调度重新缩放时间网格，吸收调度相关的增长项，缓解终端采样阶段的刚性问题；（2）累积强度外推更新规则，通过重用前一步缓存的历史模型输出，改进非均匀时间网格上步进累积强度的近似。该方法每步仅需一次NFE，不引入额外模型评估。

Result: 理论分析给出了累积强度局部近似误差的界，并证明了收敛性；在合成任务、文本生成和文本到图像基准上的广泛实验表明，TR-CIE在有限NFE下显著提升了采样质量。

Conclusion: TR-CIE为有限NFE下的DFM采样提供了高效且理论保证的解决方案，在不增加计算开销的前提下有效提升了采样质量，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching (DFM) provides a principled framework for generative modeling on discrete state spaces via continuous-time Markov chain dynamics. In practice, sampling for DFM commonly employs discretizations such as $τ$-leaping, yet efficient sampling methods under a limited number of function evaluations (NFE) remain less studied. To address this gap, we propose the Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation (TR-CIE) sampler, which aims to improve sampling quality when function evaluations are restricted. TR-CIE consists of two components. First, a schedule-based time reparameterization rescales the time grid according to the noise schedule. Under standard factorized DFM rate parameterizations, this transformation of variables absorbs the schedule-dependent growth term and mitigates stiffness near the terminal sampling stage. Second, we introduce a cumulative-intensity extrapolation updating rule. By reusing cached model outputs from the previous step as a history term, this improves the approximation of stepwise cumulative intensities on the resulting non-uniform time grid. We provide a theoretical analysis that bounds the local approximation error of cumulative intensities and establishes convergence results. The resulting sampler requires one NFE per step and introduces no additional model evaluations compared to the standard $τ$-leaping sampler. Extensive experiments on synthetic tasks, text generation, and text-to-image benchmarks demonstrate that our method improves sampling quality under limited NFE.

</details>


### [43] [AsyncOPD: How Stale Can On-Policy Distillation Be?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24143)
*Wonjun Kang,Kevin Galim,Seunghyuk Oh,Minjun Kang,Sanghyun Park,Donghoon Kim,Minjae Lee,Minseo Kim,Rishabh Tiwari,Yuchen Zeng,Hyung Il Koo,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AsyncOPD，一种异步在线策略蒸馏训练流程，通过解耦rollout生成与学习者更新，实现了1.6倍至3.8倍的训练吞吐量提升，同时保持相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在大型语言模型后训练中日益重要，但与强化学习类似，面临在线策略系统瓶颈——对于推理任务，rollout生成主导训练时间。异步训练管道可以通过解耦rollout生成与学习者更新来缓解此瓶颈，但会引入过时策略数据问题，而该问题在OPD中的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 本文首次系统研究了异步OPD中的过时性问题，关注教师反馈通过局部KL损失实现、完整词表教师logits存储/传输成本过高的实际场景。研究包括：(1) 分析KL方向对过时数据问题的影响；(2) 针对反向KL情况，研究异步RL稳定性方法是否可以缓解OPD过时性；(3) 分析有限教师分数缓存如何为稀疏和采样的反向KL OPD估计器创建偏差-方差权衡，提出多样本蒙特卡洛方法；(4) 基于这些估计器选择构建完全异步的OPD训练管道AsyncOPD。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 教师加权的前向KL对过时rollout更鲁棒，而学生加权的反向KL更脆弱；(2) 对于脆弱的反向KL情况，为异步RL设计的方法未能改善更简单的OPD特定替代方案（在学习者时重新计算反向KL信号）；(3) 多样本蒙特卡洛方法在保持MC可校正性的同时降低了单样本方差；(4) AsyncOPD在保持相当准确率的同时，训练吞吐量比严格同步训练提升了1.6倍至3.8倍。

Conclusion: 本文通过系统研究异步OPD中的过时性问题，揭示了KL方向对过时数据鲁棒性的关键影响，提出了多样本蒙特卡洛方法来平衡偏差与方差，并构建了开源的AsyncOPD训练管道，有效解决了在线策略蒸馏的系统瓶颈问题。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts guided by teacher feedback and is becoming increasingly important for large language model (LLM) post-training. Like reinforcement learning (RL), however, OPD faces an on-policy systems bottleneck, as rollouts can dominate training time for reasoning workloads. Asynchronous training pipelines can alleviate this bottleneck by decoupling rollout generation from learner updates, but doing so introduces stale-policy data. While prior work has studied stale data in asynchronous RL, its effects in OPD remain underexplored. We present the first systematic study of staleness in asynchronous OPD, focusing on a practical setting where teacher feedback is implemented through local KL losses and full-vocabulary teacher logits are too expensive to store or transfer, necessitating finite teacher-score caches. We first show that KL direction changes the stale-data problem: teacher-weighted forward KL is more robust to stale rollouts, whereas student-weighted reverse KL is vulnerable. Second, for this vulnerable reverse-KL case, we study whether methods designed to stabilize asynchronous RL can mitigate OPD staleness. In our experiments, they do not improve over a simpler OPD-specific surrogate: recomputing the reverse-KL signal under the current student at learner time. Third, we analyze how finite teacher-score caches create a bias-variance tradeoff for sparse and sampled reverse-KL OPD estimators. This motivates multi-sample Monte Carlo (MC), which preserves MC correctability while reducing one-sample variance. Finally, we present and open-source AsyncOPD, a fully asynchronous OPD training pipeline built from these estimator choices. Experiments show that AsyncOPD improves training throughput by $1.6\times$ to $3.8\times$ over strict synchronous training while reaching comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [44] [Lightweight Transformer Models for On-Device Fault Detection: A Benchmark Study on Resource-Constrained Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24173)
*Disha Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了传统机器学习与轻量级Transformer在设备端故障检测中的表现，发现在数据分离度好的场景下Transformer精度相当但计算开销大，量化与自适应推理管线能有效降低延迟，但在极端不平衡数据集上所有方法均表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的硬件上部署机器学习模型进行设备端故障检测需要在精度、延迟与模型大小之间进行权衡，现有研究缺乏针对轻量级Transformer与传统方法的系统性基准对比。

Method: 在NASA C-MAPSS涡扇退化、SECOM半导体制造和UCI AI4I 2020预测性维护三个公开数据集上，对比Random Forest、XGBoost、SVM、Logistic Regression等传统方法与DistilBERT、TinyBERT-6L、TinyBERT-4L、MobileBERT等轻量级Transformer架构，评估F1分数、AUC、模型大小和CPU推理延迟，并引入INT8动态量化与两阶段自适应推理管线。

Result: 在数据分离度好的C-MAPSS数据集上，轻量级Transformer达到87.8%的F1分数，与传统机器学习相当，但模型体积约100倍、延迟约9000倍；TinyBERT-4L为最适合部署的Transformer（55 MB，18 ms延迟）；INT8量化使模型缩小25%并保持86.9%的F1；自适应管线将97.9%的样本路由至量化分流模型，平均延迟19.5 ms时达到87.6%的F1。在SECOM和UCI-PM极端不平衡数据集上，所有方法均表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 轻量级Transformer在数据质量较好的场景下可通过量化和自适应推理管线实现高效部署，但当前方法在极端类别不平衡的故障检测任务中存在根本性局限，需进一步研究针对性解决方案。

Abstract: On-device fault detection enables real-time diagnostics without cloud dependency, but deploying machine learning models on resource-constrained hardware demands careful tradeoffs between accuracy, latency, and model size. We present a benchmark comparing traditional ML methods (Random Forest, XGBoost, SVM, Logistic Regression) against lightweight transformer architectures (DistilBERT, TinyBERT-6L, TinyBERT-4L, MobileBERT) for binary fault detection across three public datasets: NASA C-MAPSS turbofan degradation, SECOM semiconductor manufacturing, and UCI AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance. We evaluate classification performance (F1-score, AUC), model size, and CPU inference latency, and further assess INT8 dynamic quantization and a two-stage adaptive inference pipeline. Our results reveal that on well-separated sensor data (C-MAPSS), lightweight transformers match traditional ML at 87.8% F1 but at 100x the model size and 9000x the latency. TinyBERT-4L emerges as the most deployment-friendly transformer at 55 MB and 18 ms CPU latency. INT8 quantization reduces size by 25% while preserving 86.9% F1. Our adaptive pipeline, routing 97.9% of predictions through a quantized triage model and only 2.1% to a larger expert, achieves 87.6% F1 at 19.5 ms average latency. On severely imbalanced datasets (SECOM, UCI-PM), both traditional and transformer methods struggle significantly, highlighting fundamental limitations of current approaches for extreme class imbalance in fault detection. All code is publicly available.

</details>


### [45] [Project Ariadne: Prompt-Conditioned Route Generation for Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24184)
*Anton Morgunov,Victor S. Batista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ariadne，一种仅解码器的逆合成路线生成器，通过提示-完成序列统一表示目标分子、可选约束和合成路线，在约束规划任务上显著优于现有方法，同时大幅降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的逆合成规划方法通过迭代应用单步反应模型进行搜索，约束变体通常需要专门的算法或架构修改。现有的直接生成方法虽然将逆合成重构为序列生成任务，但仍需针对不同的规划规范训练单独的模型。本文旨在开发一个能够通过提示条件统一处理多种规划规范的路线生成模型。

Method: 提出Ariadne，一种仅解码器的路线生成器，将目标分子、可选约束和合成路线表示在一个提示-完成序列中。通过添加相应的提示字段（如路线深度和必需起始材料约束）来引导生成过程，实现单个模型处理多种规划规范。

Result: 在RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160基准测试上，单个24层检查点即可遵循路线深度和必需起始材料提示。添加相应提示字段使深度约束的Solv-0提高13.7分，必需叶节点约束提高31.2分。相比双向搜索规划器DESP，Ariadne在必需叶节点Top-10和Solv-0上更优，计算时间仅24 GPU分钟对比6.8 GPU小时。在标准重建任务上，Ariadne与DMS Explorer XL性能相当，但推理时间约为其一半。

Conclusion: 本工作将序列生成从专门的逆合成模型扩展到提示条件的结构化路线生成，证明了统一模型框架的有效性。作者发布了代码库和训练脚本以支持后续研究，同时指出Tier-1--3路线检查器仍是此类模型在实际化学应用中的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Retrosynthetic planning seeks to connect a target molecule to commercially available starting materials through a multistep route. Classical planners construct such routes by iteratively applying single-step reaction models within a search procedure; constrained variants often require specialized algorithms or architectural changes. Direct route generation reframes retrosynthesis as sequence generation, but existing direct-generation methods still train separate models for different planning specifications. We introduce Ariadne, a decoder-only route generator that represents the target, optional constraints, and route in one prompt-completion sequence. On the RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160 benchmark family, one 24-layer checkpoint follows route-depth and required-starting-material prompts: adding the corresponding prompt fields raises Solv-0 by 13.7 points for depth constraints and 31.2 points for required-leaf constraints. Ariadne also improves over DESP, a bidirectional search planner, on required-leaf Top-10 and Solv-0 in 24 GPU-minutes versus 6.8 GPU-hours. On standard reconstruction, Ariadne is comparable to DMS Explorer XL at about half the reported inference time. Across additional target-only benchmarks, Ariadne's clearest gains are on route-holdout reconstruction, whereas AiZynthFinder MCTS remains stronger on several Solv-0 comparisons. These results extend sequence generation from specialist retrosynthesis models to prompt-conditioned structural route generation. We release the codebase and training scripts to support further work, but do not introduce Tier-1--3 route checkers; those remain the main bottleneck before models of this kind can become useful to experimental chemists.

</details>


### [46] [Managing Task Execution for Unknown Workloads in Batteryless IoT: A Hardware-Agnostic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24340)
*Samer Nasser,Henrique Duarte Moura,Ritesh Kumar Singh,Maarten Weyn,Jeroen Famaey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对无电池物联网设备，提出了两种无需先验能量信息的硬件无关动态调度策略（强化学习和近似预测方法），揭示了不同方法的权衡取舍：近似预测实现轻量级高吞吐量，强化学习提供可调的生存-执行平衡，而大容量设备可采用简单静态策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网向无电池、能量采集架构转型，传统能量感知调度器依赖静态执行阈值或预先测量的硬件特定任务配置，难以应对不可预测的工作负载和高度波动的存储能量，亟需智能化的能量管理策略来维持系统可靠运行。

Method: 提出两种硬件无关的动态调度策略：1）无模型强化学习(RL)智能体，将应用视为"黑盒"；2）实时近似预测(AP)方法。构建基于真实太阳能数据和动态LoRa传输配置的物理精确仿真框架，与自适应任务速率方法(AsTAR)和优化静态阈值进行对比评估。

Result: AP方法以轻量级计算开销实现了接近理论最优的任务吞吐量；RL智能体提供可调节的生存-执行平衡能力；AsTAR在跨越长期能量间隙的执行节奏控制方面表现优异；大容量电容器设备可有效依赖简单、低计算成本的静态策略。

Conclusion: 不同调度策略各有优势，需根据系统约束选择：严重受限的小容量系统需要高级策略提供关键韧性，而大能量缓冲设备可采用更简单的静态策略。研究表明硬件无关策略可有效解决无电池物联网的能量管理挑战，为实际部署提供了策略选择指导。

Abstract: In recent years, the Internet of Things (IoT) paradigm has been shifting toward batteryless, energy-harvesting architectures. Sustaining reliable operation in these systems requires intelligent management of highly volatile stored energy. As edge applications grow in complexity, traditional energy-aware schedulers struggle with unpredictable workloads due to their reliance on static execution thresholds or pre-measured, hardware-specific task profiles. To overcome this, we propose two novel, hardware-agnostic dynamic scheduling strategies treating applications as a "black box," requiring no prior energy information: a model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent and an on-the-fly Approximated Prediction (AP) method. We evaluate these methods against an adaptive task rate approach (AsTAR) and optimized static thresholds using a custom-built, physically accurate simulation framework driven by real-world solar data and dynamic LoRa transmission profiles. Rather than claiming universal superiority, our analysis exposes the distinct operational trade-offs of each method: the AP approach delivers lightweight, near-oracle task throughput; the RL agent provides tunable survival-execution balancing; and AsTAR excels at execution pacing across long energy gaps. Finally, we demonstrate that while these advanced strategies provide critical resilience for severely constrained systems with small capacitors, devices with larger energy buffers can efficiently rely on simpler, less computationally expensive static policies.

</details>


### [47] [Parallel Manifold Steering: Efficient Adaptation of Large Associative Memories via Residual Energy Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24396)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出H-Res方法，通过在激活流形上学习状态依赖的向量场来调制Transformer的能量景观，在不修改权重或扩展序列长度的情况下实现高效适配，性能提升26%且无计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型Transformer模型作为稠密联想记忆系统，在面对新任务时存在"可塑性-稳定性"困境：现有方法要么通过直接修改权重（如LoRA）导致灾难性干扰，要么通过静态提示令牌（如VPT）堵塞检索缓冲区从而降低联想容量。

Method: 提出H-Res（分层残差引导）机制，将适配问题形式化为激活流形上的控制问题。该方法学习一个状态依赖的向量场，在保持全局平衡和序列长度不变的前提下，引导令牌轨迹进入任务特定的吸引子域，从而调制Transformer的有效能量景观。

Result: 理论上证明H-Res保持了基础模型的注意力熵并促进神经坍塌现象。实验上，该方法在联想检索任务上比全局权重修改方法性能提升26%，消除了基于提示方法的计算开销，并能有效扩展到结构化领域。

Conclusion: H-Res成功解决了Transformer适配中的可塑性-稳定性困境，通过在激活层面进行控制而非修改权重或扩展序列，实现了更高效的模型适配，为冻结记忆系统的灵活适应提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Transformer models function as Dense Associative Memories (DAMs), retrieving knowledge via high-dimensional attractor dynamics driven by the self-attention mechanism \citep{ramsauer2020hopfield, wu2024attention}. However, adapting these frozen memory systems to new tasks presents a fundamental ``Plasticity-Stability'' dilemma. Current methods either risk catastrophic interference by modifying synaptic weights directly (e.g., LoRA) \citep{hu2021lora} or degrade associative capacity by clogging the retrieval buffer with static prompt tokens (e.g., VPT) \citep{jia2022vpt}. In this work, we propose \textbf{H-Res} (Hierarchical Residual Steering), a mechanism that modulates the effective energy landscape of the Transformer without altering its global equilibrium or expanding its sequence length. By formulating adaptation as a control problem on the activation manifold \citep{chen2018neuralode}, H-Res learns a state-dependent vector field that steers token trajectories into task-specific basins of attraction. We formally prove that H-Res preserves the attention entropy of the foundation model and facilitates Neural Collapse \citep{papyan2020prevalence}. Empirically, Manifold Steering outperforms global weight modification by 26\% on associative retrieval tasks and eliminates the computational overhead of prompt-based methods, scaling effectively to structured domains \citep{zha2023vtab}.

</details>


### [48] [Natural Identifiers for Privacy and Data Audits in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24408)
*Lorenzo Rossi,Bartłomiej Marek,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了自然标识符（NIDs），利用训练数据中天然存在的结构化随机字符串（如加密哈希、短网址）来实现LLM的事后差分隐私审计和数据集推断，无需模型重训练或额外的隐私非成员数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM隐私审计方法存在两大局限：(1) 差分隐私审计需要在训练中插入金丝雀数据，对已训练模型进行审计需昂贵的重训练；(2) 数据集推断需要访问与嫌疑数据同分布的隐私非成员数据集，这在现实场景中往往难以获取。这些限制严重阻碍了可扩展的事后审计。

Method: 引入自然标识符（NIDs）作为新方案。NIDs是LLM训练数据集中自然存在的结构化随机字符串（如加密哈希、缩短URL）。其格式特性允许从相同分布生成无限多的随机字符串，这些字符串可作为审计的替代金丝雀，以及数据集推断的同分布非成员数据。

Result: 评估表明，利用NIDs可以：(1) 无需任何重训练即可实现事后差分隐私审计；(2) 对任何包含NIDs的嫌疑数据集进行数据集推断，无需隐私非成员数据集。

Conclusion: NIDs成功解决了LLM事后隐私审计的核心障碍，实现了无需重训练的差分隐私审计和无需额外数据集的数据集推断，为可扩展的事后审计提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Assessing the privacy of large language models (LLMs) presents significant challenges. In particular, most existing methods for auditing differential privacy require the insertion of specially crafted canary data during training, making them impractical for auditing already-trained models without costly retraining. Additionally, dataset inference, which audits whether a suspect dataset was used to train a model, is infeasible without access to a private non-member held-out dataset. Yet, such held-out datasets are often unavailable or difficult to construct for real-world cases since they have to be from the same distribution (IID) as the suspect data. These limitations severely hinder the ability to conduct scalable, post-hoc audits. To enable such audits, this work introduces natural identifiers (NIDs) as a novel solution to the above-mentioned challenges. NIDs are structured random strings, such as cryptographic hashes and shortened URLs, naturally occurring in common LLM training datasets. Their format enables the generation of unlimited additional random strings from the same distribution, which can act as alternative canaries for audits and as same-distribution held-out data for dataset inference. Our evaluation highlights that indeed, using NIDs, we can facilitate post-hoc differential privacy auditing without any retraining and enable dataset inference for any suspect dataset containing NIDs without the need for a private non-member held-out dataset.

</details>


### [49] [Data Augmentation: A Fourier Analysis Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24418)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大数据增强群体导致的计算瓶颈，证明基于随机采样的部分数据增强能达到与完全增强相同的统计最优率，为利用群不变性进行可扩展学习提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强通过群作用实现对已知不变性的利用，但在群较大时完全增强的计算代价过高。核心问题在于：部分数据增强是否能在泛化与样本复杂度方面获得与完全增强相同的统计收益？

Method: 基于傅里叶分析与有限群表示论的一般理论框架，研究部分增强（从群中随机采样子集进行增强）与完全增强的统计性质，并刻画精确不变性所需的条件。

Result: 对经典学习问题，基于随机采样的部分数据增强能达到与完全增强相同的最小极大速率，逼近误差随子集规模增大趋于零；但当假设空间充分表达时，仅通过对群的真子集平均无法实现精确不变性，必须对整个群平均。

Conclusion: 部分数据增强以近似对称方式实现了与完全增强相同的统计最优性，提供了精确与近似对称强制以及完全与部分增强的统一视角，回答了在一般群不变性下是否可用可扩展方法达到统计最优学习的问题。

Abstract: Data augmentation is a simple and model-agnostic approach for exploiting known invariances in learning problems. Given a group acting on the input space, one augments the training set with transformed copies of each sample. Because it exploits symmetries without modifying the underlying learning algorithm, data augmentation can be applied broadly across learning methods. However, this universality comes at a computational cost: when the group is large, full group-sized augmentation quickly becomes computationally infeasible. This raises a fundamental question: Can partial data augmentation achieve the same statistical benefits as full augmentation in terms of generalization and sample complexity? We develop a general framework for investigating this question using Fourier analysis and the representation theory of finite groups. We show that, for a broad class of classical learning problems, partial data augmentation based on a randomly sampled subset of group elements achieves the same minimax rates as full augmentation, up to an approximation error that vanishes as the subset size increases. Our results provide a theoretical explanation for why partial augmentation can retain the statistical benefits of full augmentation despite enforcing symmetry only approximately, and shed light on a recently raised question in learning with symmetries: whether statistically optimal learning under general group invariances can be achieved using computationally scalable methods. Moreover, we prove a complementary impossibility result: enforcing exact invariance via data augmentation requires averaging over the entire group, and cannot be achieved by any strict subset when the hypothesis space is sufficiently expressive. Together, these results provide a unified perspective on full and partial data augmentation, as well as exact and approximate symmetry enforcement.

</details>


### [50] [An LLM-based Two-Stage Transformer Framework for Cross-Domain Bearing Fault Diagnosis with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24459)
*Jinghan Wang,Feng Cheng,Wentao Wu,Hang Li,Gaoliang Peng,Tianchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架，解决了数据集异构性、工况变化和标注数据稀缺同时存在的挑战，在四个真实数据集上以10%标注数据达到92.61%平均准确率，超越现有方法17.24个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中轴承故障诊断面临数据集异构性、工况变化和标注数据稀缺三大挑战同时发生的困境。现有方法孤立地解决这些问题，且依赖隐式特征对齐，在并发挑战下效果受限。

Method: 提出知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架：(1)采用轻量化GPT-2风格Transformer（含因果自注意力机制）从振动信号中提取层次化特征；(2)构建显式知识传递路径，将预训练编码器权重和故障原型嵌入作为知识载体；(3)通过多源学习获得泛化表示、基于原型的知识调制实现目标域适应、分类法自适应分类实现跨异构故障类别无缝迁移。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上验证，仅使用10%标注目标数据即可达到92.61%平均准确率，超越当前最先进方法17.24个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了并发挑战下的轴承故障诊断问题，为工业4.0应用中具有成本效益的预测性维护提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Bearing fault diagnosis faces critical challenges when dataset heterogeneity, operating condition variations, and limited labeled data occur simultaneously in industrial environments. Existing approaches address these issues in isolation and rely on implicit feature alignment, limiting effectiveness under concurrent challenges. This paper proposes a knowledge-guided two-stage transfer learning framework that employs a lightweight GPT-2-style Transformer with causal self-attention for hierarchical feature extraction from vibration signals, establishing explicit pathways where pre-trained encoder weights and fault prototype embeddings serve as knowledge carriers from multi-source pre-training to target adaptation. The framework addresses the dual-shift challenge through multi-source learning for generalizable representations, prototype-based knowledge modulation for target adaptation, and taxonomy-adaptive classification for seamless transfer across heterogeneous fault categories. Experimental validation on four real-world datasets demonstrates 92.61% average accuracy with only 10% labeled target data, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 17.24 percentage points, establishing a practical pathway toward cost-effective predictive maintenance in Industry 4.0 applications.

</details>


### [51] [A Fair Evaluation of Graph Foundation Models for Node Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24509)
*Oleg Platonov,Gleb Bazhenov,Dmitry Eremeev,Liudmila Prokhorenkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对9个用于节点属性预测的图基础模型（GFMs）进行公平严格的重评估，发现仅基于预数据拟合网络（Prior-data Fitted Networks）的最新模型在预测性能上优于调优良好的GNN基线，但推理成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型（GFMs）在工业和科学领域应用广泛，节点属性预测是图机器学习中最热门的任务之一。然而，现有GFMs评估方式差异巨大，缺乏统一评估设置，导致无法可靠比较GFMs之间及其与其他模型的性能。

Method: 对9个最新的用于节点属性预测的图基础模型进行公平且严格的重评估，并将其与强力的图神经网络（GNN）基线模型进行对比。

Result: 在被评估的GFMs中，只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新模型在预测性能上超过了良好调优的GNN，但这些模型的推理成本更高。

Conclusion: 当前GFMs领域缺乏统一评估标准，需要建立公平严谨的评价体系；基于Prior-data Fitted Networks的GFM在性能上展现优势，但存在推理效率的权衡问题。

Abstract: Due to the wide use of graph-structured data in different fields of industry and science, the development of Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) has recently attracted a lot of attention. While many different types of models are called GFMs, particular interest has been paid to GFMs designed for node property prediction tasks, which is one of the most popular settings in Graph ML with lots of real-world applications from fraud detection in financial and social networks to recommendation systems for e-commerce and user-generated content platforms. While a number of GFMs for this task have been recently proposed, the field has not converged to a unified evaluation setting, and different works evaluate their models in widely different ways, preventing reliable comparison of GFMs with each other and with other types of models. In this work, we conduct a fair and rigorous reevaluation of 9 recent GFMs for node property prediction, comparing them to strong Graph Neural Network (GNN) baselines. We find that, among these GFMs, only the most recent ones based on the Prior-data Fitted Networks paradigm outperform well-tuned GNNs in predictive performance, although at a higher inference cost.

</details>


### [52] [Reasoning as Attractor Dynamics: Latent Memory Retrieval via Gibbs-Weighted Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24543)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理学的检索机制，通过采样多个推理路径并按逆能量加权，将LLM推理视为向吸引子盆地松弛的动态过程，在GSM8K上将Phi-3.5性能提升5.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM被视为自回归生成器，但从集体计算视角看，它们作为高维稠密联想记忆存储复杂推理模式。作者假设正确推理链对应能量景观中深而宽的吸引子盆地（平坦极小值），而幻觉则表现为尖锐不稳定的局部极小值，旨在利用这种几何结构改进推理。

Method: 引入基于轨迹谱熵吉布斯测度的检索机制。通过采样多条推理路径并按逆能量加权（$P \propto e^{-βE}$），近似联想记忆的平衡分布，将系统有效“松弛”至稳健解，而非贪婪预测下一token。

Result: 该方法在Microsoft Phi-3.5模型上，于GSM8K基准测试中将性能从84.7%提升至90.1%，绝对提升5.38%。实验证明将推理建模为向吸引子盆地动态沉降过程优于贪婪下一token预测。

Conclusion: LLM的推理过程更适合被建模为向能量景观中吸引子盆地动态沉降的过程，而非简单的贪婪下一token预测。这种受物理学启发的机制有效利用了模型的联想记忆特性，显著提升了数学推理性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are traditionally viewed as autoregressive generators. However, from the perspective of collective computation, they function as high-dimensional Dense Associative Memories that store complex reasoning patterns as latent attractors. In this work, we investigate the energy landscape of mathematical reasoning. We posit that correct reasoning chains correspond to deep, wide attractor basins ("flat minima") in the model's output distribution, whereas hallucinations manifest as sharp, unstable local minima. To exploit this geometry, we introduce a retrieval mechanism based on a Gibbs measure of the trajectory's spectral entropy. By sampling multiple reasoning paths and weighting them by their inverse energy ($P \propto e^{-βE}$), we approximate the equilibrium distribution of the associative memory, effectively ``relaxing'' the system into a robust solution. Empirically, this physics-inspired mechanism improves Microsoft Phi-3.5 performance on GSM8K by 5.38\% (84.7\% $\to$ 90.1\%), demonstrating that inference is better modeled as a dynamic settling process into an attractor basin rather than greedy next-token prediction.

</details>


### [53] [QC-SMOTE: Quality-Controlled SMOTE for Imbalanced Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24625)
*Parth Upman,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QC-SMOTE框架，通过质量控制和几何自适应策略生成高质量合成样本，有效解决类别不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如SMOTE）在噪声或类别重叠区域容易生成低质量合成样本，影响分类性能。

Method: 提出QC-SMOTE框架：(1)使用综合邻域可信度分数（结合局部密度、安全级别和与多数类隔离度）估计少数类样本可靠性；(2)采用IPQ引导的最佳K策略生成合成候选样本，评估中点纯度并根据需要进行多数类清理；(3)根据样本可靠性和边界信息性指导样本分配；(4)在重叠-不平衡情境下自适应调整插值范围和选择标准；(5)当邻域纯度低于自适应阈值时，用原始少数类样本副本替换低质量合成样本。

Result: 在30个不平衡数据集上使用重复分层交叉验证实验表明，QC-SMOTE在比较的过采样方法中实现了最强的平均AUC-ROC和Macro F1，特别是在中度和严重不平衡情境下优势明显。

Conclusion: 质量感知和几何自适应的合成采样对鲁棒的不平衡分类具有重要意义。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a significant challenge in classification, where existing methods such as SMOTE often generate low-quality synthetic samples in regions with noise or class overlap. We propose QC-SMOTE, a quality-controlled oversampling framework that estimates minority sample reliability using a composite neighbourhood trustworthiness score combining local density, safe-level, and isolation from the majority class. Synthetic candidates are generated using an IPQ-guided best-of-K strategy that evaluates midpoint purity and, when required, majority clearance, with allocation guided by sample reliability and boundary informativeness. Generation behaviour adapts across overlap--imbalance regimes, adjusting interpolation range and selection criteria to match local data geometry. Low-quality synthetic samples are replaced with original minority duplicates when neighbourhood purity falls below an adaptive threshold, providing graceful degradation by reverting to duplication in severely noisy regions. Experiments on 30 imbalanced datasets using repeated stratified cross-validation show that QC-SMOTE achieves the strongest average AUC-ROC and Macro F1 among the compared oversampling methods, with particularly clear gains under moderate and severe imbalance. These results demonstrate the importance of quality-aware, geometry-adaptive synthetic sampling for robust imbalanced classification.

</details>


### [54] [FlowPipe: LLM-Enhanced Conditional Generative Flow Networks for Data Preparation Pipeline Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24679)
*Kunyu Ni,Lei Cao,Jie He,Xiaotong Zhang,Jianfeng Jin,Junyu Dong,Yanwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowPipe框架，将数据准备流水线构建问题建模为有向无环图上的条件概率流生成，利用C-GFlowNets、深度语义调制和失效感知机制，在74个数据集上平均精度提升11.96%，训练收敛速度提升12.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA多DQN方法在自动构建数据准备流水线时面临三个关键局限：解耦的价值估计器削弱长期信用分配、数据集上下文仅弱注入到策略中、在包含大量无效状态的稀疏搜索空间中探索效率低下。

Method: FlowPipe将流水线合成形式化为有向无环图上的条件概率流生成，采用条件生成流网络（C-GFlowNets）与轨迹平衡目标，将终端验证奖励与早期流水线决策关联；通过特征线性调制（FiLM）引入深度语义调制，利用LLM导出的逻辑先验根据数据集语义调节策略的内部激活；将失效感知融入流目标以避免无效状态并集中于高潜力区域。

Result: 在包含74个真实世界数据集的两个基准测试套件上，FlowPipe超越SOTA基线方法，平均准确率提升11.96%，训练收敛速度提升12.5倍。

Conclusion: FlowPipe通过统一的条件概率流生成框架有效解决了多DQN方法的局限性，显著提升了数据准备流水线自动构建的性能与效率。

Abstract: Data preparation pipelines improve data quality in machine learning by transforming raw tables into learning-ready data through sequential cleaning and feature transformation operators. However, automatically constructing such pipelines is computationally difficult because operator sequences are combinatorial and end-to-end evaluation is expensive. Existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) Multi-DQN methods still face three key limitations: decoupled value estimators weaken long-horizon credit assignment, dataset context is only weakly injected into the policy, and exploration is inefficient in a sparse search space with many invalid states. To address these issues, we propose FlowPipe, a unified framework that formulates pipeline synthesis as conditional probabilistic flow generation over a directed acyclic graph. FlowPipe uses Conditional Generative Flow Networks (C-GFlowNets) with a Trajectory Balance objective to connect terminal validation rewards with early pipeline decisions. It further introduces Deep Semantic Modulation through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), allowing LLM-derived logical priors to condition the policy's internal activations according to dataset semantics. In addition, FlowPipe incorporates failure awareness into the flow objective to avoid invalid states and concentrate search on high-potential regions. Experiments on two benchmark suites with 74 real-world datasets show that FlowPipe outperforms SOTA baselines, improving accuracy by 11.96% on average and achieving 12.5x faster training convergence. Source code is available at https://github.com/KunyuNi/FlowPipe.

</details>


### [55] [Grad Detect: Gradient-Based Hallucination Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24790)
*Anand Kamat,Daniel Blake,Brent M. Werness*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Grad Detect方法，通过分析推理过程中的层级梯度模式来检测大语言模型的幻觉，发现最后五层集中了97%以上的判别性梯度信号，在幻觉检测和模型弃权预测任务上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然在多样化任务中表现出色，但仍然容易产生幻觉。在高风险应用中部署LLM时，检测这些幻觉对于确保模型可靠性至关重要，而仅依靠输出级别的信号无法充分捕捉模型输出的正确性信息。

Method: 提出Grad Detect方法，通过在推理过程中进行单次前向-后向传播，分析层级梯度模式来预测幻觉。该方法利用模型内部梯度结构携带的关于输出正确性的丰富信息，并在四个架构家族的十一个模型上进行全面的层级消融研究。

Result: 在多个问答基准测试中，Grad Detect在幻觉检测和模型弃权预测任务上始终优于基于置信度和基于采样的基线方法。研究发现最后五层集中了超过97%的判别性梯度信号，使得该方法能够在性能损失最小的情况下高效部署。

Conclusion: Grad Detect提供了一个统一的框架来预测LLM可靠性的多个维度，不仅具有强大的预测性能，还能提供关于模型故障起源位置和方式的可解释洞察，为LLM的可靠部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse tasks, yet they remain prone to generating hallucinations. Detecting these hallucinations is critical for deploying LLMs reliably in high-stakes applications. We present Grad Detect, a gradient-based approach for predicting hallucinations by analyzing layer-wise gradient patterns from a single forward-backward pass during inference. Our method shows that the internal gradient structure of a model carries rich information about the correctness of its output. This information is not accessible through output-level signals alone. We evaluate Grad Detect on several Q&A benchmarks across both hallucination detection and model abstention prediction, where it consistently outperforms confidence-based and sampling-based baselines. Through comprehensive layer ablation studies across all eleven models from four architectural families, we find that the final five layers concentrate over 97% of the discriminative gradient signal, enabling efficient deployment with minimal performance loss. Grad Detect provides a unified framework for predicting multiple dimensions of LLM reliability, offering strong predictive performance alongside interpretable insights into where and how model failures originate.

</details>


### [56] [Real vs. Complex Spectral Bases for Neural Operators: The Role of Green's Function Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24851)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了实值的Hartley神经算子(HNO)作为复数FNO的实值镜像，发现椭圆型PDE适合HNO，含相位的时间依赖型PDE适合FNO，关键在于谱基与解算子对称性相匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO在处理实值PDE解时存在复数表示的冗余性（共轭对称性），作者希望探索实值变换能否消除这种冗余，并研究不同谱基对不同类型PDE的适用性。

Method: 提出HNO，用实值的离散Hartley变换替代FFT，每个频谱模式只需学习一个实乘数，无需复数运算。在相同参数量下，对椭圆型算子（Poisson、双调和）和时间依赖型算子（波动、平流、Burgers、Navier-Stokes）进行系统对比实验。

Result: 发现椭圆型PDE（具有实对称Green函数）适合HNO，而含相位的时间依赖型PDE更适合FNO，这种性能分裂与算子的相位含量单调相关，热传导方程作为边界情况。实验结果与Green函数理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优方法，应根据解算子的对称性选择谱基：实对称算子选HNO，含相位算子选FNO，这提供了可预测的选择规则。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) learn solution operators of partial differential equations by parameterizing global convolutions in the complex Fourier domain. For real-valued PDE solutions, the complex FFT carries representational redundancy through conjugate symmetry. We introduce the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), the exact real-valued mirror of FNO: it replaces the FFT with the purely real Discrete Hartley Transform and learns a single real multiplier per retained spectral mode, with no complex arithmetic. Because the real Hartley spectrum is not halved by conjugate symmetry, HNO retains twice as many frequency corners as FNO but one real weight where FNO carries a complex pair, so the two operators are iso-parametric at equal width and differ only in spectral basis. Our central thesis is that the best basis is a property of the operator. Self-adjoint elliptic operators (Poisson, biharmonic) have real, symmetric Green's functions that the real Hartley multiplier diagonalizes exactly, and HNO is favored there. Time-dependent operators carry phase, from oscillation in the wave equation to transport in advection, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, which a real diagonal multiplier cannot represent, so FNO is favored there, and increasingly so with the operator's phase content, leaving the phaseless heat equation as the borderline case. Training both operators identically and benchmarking across PDE classes, initial-condition families, and boundary conditions, we find an elliptic-versus-time-dependent split that is monotone in operator phase content and matches the Green's-function theory we develop. Rather than a universal winner, our findings give a predictive rule: match the spectral basis to the symmetry of the solution operator.

</details>

### [57] [PCB-QA: Evaluating LLMs over the First Printed Circuit Board Design Question-Answer Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23704)
*Sahana Srinivasan,Benjamin Tan,Benjamin Turnbull,Hammond Pearce*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个PCB问答数据集PCB-QA（480个问答对），并 benchmark 了4个大模型。研究发现Gemini 3 Flash Preview在使用JSON文本格式时达到93%准确率，验证了LLM评估文本化PCB设计文件的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在集成电路EDA领域已展现出能力，但在PCB设计与分析任务中的应用仍缺乏探索。主要瓶颈在于：（1）缺乏基于文本的PCB数据集来评估LLM；（2）缺乏针对不同类型PCB设计文件的LLM提示方法学。

Method: 提出PCB-QA数据集：手动创建480个问答对，源自8个不同复杂度的开源硬件项目，涵盖元件连接、数据手册检查和SPICE仿真数据。使用多种PCB设计文件表示形式（及其组合）提示LLM，测试模型是否能理解原理图和网表的“原生形式”（如图形PDF、KiCAD格式设计文件）或更偏好文本格式。对4个SOTA LLM（商业和开源模型）进行基准测试。

Result: 在4个SOTA LLM基准测试中，Gemini 3 Flash Preview使用提出的JSON文本格式达到93%的准确率。证明了基于文本的PCB设计格式可以被LLM有效评估，同时首次测量了LLM理解原生PCB设计文件格式的能力。

Conclusion: 本研究开源的问答数据集是LLM集成到PCB设计生命周期中的第一步，证明了文本化PCB设计格式可以有效地被大语言模型评估，为后续LLM在PCB设计领域的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated capabilities in electronic design automation (EDA) for integrated circuits. However, their applications in printed circuit board (PCB) design and analysis tasks remain underexplored. In part, this is due to a (1) a lack of text-based PCB datasets to evaluate LLMs and (2) a lack of methodologies for prompting LLMs with different types of PCB design files. To address this gap, our paper proposes PCB-QA: a manually created questionnaire dataset amounting to 480 question-answer pairs for PCBs, derived from 8 different open-source hardware projects of varying complexities. We examine multiple aspects of PCB designs and cover questions about component connections, datasheet examination, and simulation data obtainable via SPICE. Using our dataset as a benchmark, we prompt LLMs with different representations (and combinations) of PCB design files and record observations. This allows us to measure, for the first time, if LLMs can understand schematics and netlists in their "native forms" (i.e. graphical PDFs, KiCAD-format design files) or if textual formats are preferred. Including both commercial and open-weight models, we benchmark 4 state-of-the-art LLMs on our dataset, finding that Gemini 3 Flash Preview can answer questions with an accuracy of 93% using a proposed JSON-based textual format. This demonstrates that text-based PCB design formats can be evaluated by LLMs. Our open-source questionnaire is the first step towards enabling LLM integrations within the PCB design life cycle.

</details>


### [58] [VeriPilot: An LLM-Powered Verilog Debugging Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23759)
*Yihan Wang,Cheng Liu,Jiazheng Zhang,Lei Zhang,Long Cheng,Xiaowei Li,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出VeriPilot框架，利用黄金参考模型实现细粒度Verilog错误定位与修复，将GPT-4o修复成功率从54.3%提升至85.71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的Verilog调试方法仅依赖测试输出和编译器反馈，在处理复杂错误时效果有限。错误根源与可观测输出相距甚远，LLM难以追踪代码中的长依赖链；大规模代码库中的长上下文进一步阻碍了高效推理。

Method: 提出VeriPilot框架：1) 通过LLM分析对齐Verilog设计与黄金模型之间的内部变量语义；2) 利用静态分析生成的控制数据流图(CDFG)进行逐步信号追踪；3) 识别最小可疑代码区域及其在黄金模型中的正确对应部分；4) 将结构化洞察提供给LLM以指导推理和自动代码修复。

Result: 在NVIDIA的CVDP基准测试上，VeriPilot将GPT-4o的修复成功率从54.3%提升至85.71%，显著提高了复杂Verilog设计的错误定位精度和修复效果。

Conclusion: 通过结合黄金参考模型、语义对齐和基于CDFG的信号追踪，VeriPilot有效解决了LLM在Verilog调试中面临的长依赖链追踪和大规模代码库推理难题，实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Verilog debugging remains one of the most time-consuming stages in digital circuit design. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled automated debugging; however, most existing approaches rely solely on test outputs and compiler feedback in an end-to-end manner, limiting their effectiveness on complex bugs. A key challenge is that the root cause of an error may be far removed from its observable outputs, making it difficult for LLMs to trace long dependency chains in code. This challenge is further exacerbated in large codebases, where long context lengths hinder efficient reasoning. To address these limitations, we propose VeriPilot, an LLM-powered debugging framework that leverages golden reference models to enable fine-grained bug localization and repair. VeriPilot goes beyond output-level comparison by aligning internal variable semantics between the Verilog design and its corresponding golden model through LLM-based analysis. It then performs step-by-step signal tracing using Control-Data-Flow Graphs (CDFGs) derived from static analysis, identifying a minimal set of suspicious code regions along with their correct counterparts from the golden model. These structured insights are subsequently provided to the LLM to guide reasoning and automated code repair. Experimental results on the Comprehensive Verilog Design Problems (CVDP) benchmark from NVIDIA demonstrate that VeriPilot improves the repair success rate of GPT-4o from 54.3\% to 85.71\%, significantly enhancing both bug localization accuracy and repair effectiveness for complex Verilog designs. The source code and benchmark are publicly available at Github https://github.com/YihanWn/VeriPilot.git.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [59] [RIFT-Bench: Dynamic Red-teaming For Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23927)
*Yarin Yerushalmi Levi,Roy Betser,Amit Giloni,Lidor Erez,Itay Gershon,Oren Rachmil,Sindhu Padakandla,Roman Vainshtein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了RIFT-Bench，一种基于图表示的动态红队测试方法论，用于统一评估异构LLM驱动的智能体AI系统安全性。该方法在45个智能体系统上验证有效，并支持缓解策略评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全评估往往局限于特定实现或领域，难以对异构智能体系统进行统一比较；而基于LLM的智能体系统正快速演变为自主决策系统，暴露出超越传统LLM漏洞的新型攻击向量，亟需统一的安全评估方法。

Method: 提出了基于图表示的RIFT-Bench方法论，采用新型层次化表示，包含两个自动化阶段：Discovery阶段提取系统结构，Scanning阶段部署自适应对抗攻击并生成综合评估报告。该方法使用跨多种攻击向量和目标的动态可适配对抗探针。

Result: 在45个涵盖多样化实现的智能体系统上验证了方法的有效性，证明该方法能有效地泛化到异构智能体架构。此外，RIFT-Bench还支持对缓解策略的直接评估。

Conclusion: RIFT-Bench为智能体AI系统的安全评估提供了可扩展的基础框架，具备跨异构架构泛化能力和缓解策略评估支持等关键能力。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems powered by large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into autonomous decision-making systems, exposing attack vectors beyond those of traditional LLM vulnerabilities. Existing security evaluations are often tied to specific implementations or domains, limiting unified comparison across heterogeneous systems. To address this gap, we introduce RIFT-Bench, a graph representation-driven methodology for dynamic red-teaming that enables unified evaluations across diverse agentic architectures. Building on a novel hierarchical representation, RIFT-Bench operates in two automated phases: Discovery, which extracts system structure, and Scanning, which deploys adaptive adversarial attacks and produces a comprehensive evaluation report. It evaluates the examined system itself, leveraging a broad set of dynamically adaptable adversarial probes across diverse attack vectors and objectives. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed evaluation pipeline across 45 agentic systems spanning a diverse range of implementations, showing that the approach generalizes effectively to heterogeneous agentic architectures. Beyond systems and attacks, RIFT-Bench also supports direct evaluation of mitigation strategies. These key capabilities make RIFT-Bench a scalable foundation for security evaluation of agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [60] [Neuro-Symbolic Drive: Rule-Grounded Faithful Reasoning for Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23938)
*Xiangbo Gao,Xiukun Huang,Boyu Lu,Junge Zhang,Mengjie Mao,Jiachen Li,Wei Xiong,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Neuro-Symbolic Drive框架，通过从经典基于规则的规划器中提取规则推理轨迹来监督驾驶VLA模型，显著降低了轨迹预测误差（ADE@3s从0.47降至0.26）和miss rate（从8.30%降至6.40%），实现了推理与动作生成的结构性耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前结合链式思维(CoT)推理的驾驶VLA模型虽然利用了预训练视觉语言模型表示并以自然语言暴露中间决策，但现有推理往往缺乏确保推理与规划运动保持因果联系所需的逐步决策语义。

Method: 提出Neuro-Symbolic Drive神经符号驾驶框架：将基于规则的规划器视为符号AI推理引擎，在仿真中对其仪器化以捕获执行轨迹和每步规则评估的内部决策轨迹；将每个轨迹序列化为结构化规则推理，与轨迹配对后微调Qwen3.5-4B作为驾驶VLA。由于这些轨迹直接源自决定动作的规划器状态，确保推理与运动生成在构造上结构性耦合，而非事后对齐。

Result: 在仿真生成的基准测试中，详细规则推理在三摄像头感知下将ADE@3s从0.47降至0.26，miss rate从8.30%降至6.40%；在八摄像头感知下将ADE@3s从0.54降至0.26，miss rate从10.13%降至5.99%。

Conclusion: Neuro-Symbolic Drive成功将神经符号规划逻辑转化为结构化监督，通过从规划器状态直接导出的推理轨迹确保推理与运动生成的结构耦合，为驾驶VLA模型提供了因果关联的决策语义。

Abstract: Driving VLA models incorporating Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning are attractive because they leverage pretrained VLM representations and expose intermediate decisions in natural language, yet current rationales often lack the step-by-step decision semantics needed to keep the rationale causally connected to the planned motion. We introduce Neuro-Symbolic Drive, a neuro-symbolic driving framework that supervises a driving VLA with rule-grounded reasoning traces extracted directly from classical rule-based planners. Our key observation is that rule-based planners are symbolic AI systems that already function as executable reasoning engines: they reason about active safety constraints, search over candidate maneuvers, and select a final trajectory. We instrument these planners in simulation to capture both the executed trajectory and the internal decision trace at each rule-evaluation step. Each trace is serialized into structured rule-grounded reasoning and paired with the trajectory to fine-tune Qwen3.5-4B as a driving VLA. Because these traces are derived directly from the planner states that determine the action, they ensure reasoning is structurally coupled to motion generation by construction, rather than by post-hoc alignment. On our simulator-generated benchmark, detailed rule-grounded reasoning reduces ADE@3s from 0.47 to 0.26 and miss rate from 8.30% to 6.40% under three-camera perception, and from 0.54 to 0.26 and 10.13% to 5.99% under eight-camera perception. Neuro-Symbolic Drive thus converts neuro-symbolic planning logic into structured supervision. Code base: https://github.com/XiangboGaoBarry/Neural-Symbolic-Drive.

</details>


### [61] [Critique of Agent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23991)
*Eric Xing,Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文区分了"代理式"(agentic)与"代理性"(agentive)系统，提出真正智能体需将目标、身份、决策、自我调节和学习五个维度内化于系统内部，并提出了目标-身份-配置器(GIC)通用智能体架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型系统被营销为"编程智能体"、"AI合作科学家"等工具，同时存在AI脱离人类控制的"机器代理"忧虑，亟需厘清自动化与代理的边界，既要构建能力强大的系统，又要理解应恐惧什么。

Method: 借鉴笛卡尔将代理根植于独立思考的哲学基础，结合科幻作品中对自主存在的描绘，调研当前AI智能体现状，从目标、身份、决策、自我调节和学习五个维度分析智能体架构。

Result: 提出代理式系统(能力源于工程化工作流)与代理性系统(能力内生产生，包括社交交互)的区分，界定了为规定任务设计的系统与能在开放世界中真正自主运行的系统的边界；提出目标-身份-配置器(GIC)通用智能体架构，结合分层目标分解、身份演化、基于独立训练世界模型的模拟推理、学习的自我调节、以及从真实和模拟经验中的自主学习。

Conclusion: 分析了具有更大自主性和"代理性"但仍在人类监督下的代理性系统的可审计性、可控性和安全性，为智能体系统的发展提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: What is an agent? What constitutes agency? With the rise of Large Language Model (LLM) systems marketed as ``coding agents'', ``AI co-scientists'', and other ``agentic" tools that promise to drive up productivity, and at the same time, ``existential" concerns such as AI escaping human control with destructive power under a speculative ``machine agency" against humans, it has become essential to clarify where automation ends and agency begins, both for building capable systems and for understanding whether and what to fear. Drawing on Descartes' grounding of agency in independent thought, and on portrayals of autonomous beings in science fiction, we survey the current landscape of AI agents, and analyze agent architectures along five dimensions: goal, identity, decision-making, self-regulation, and learning. Specifically, we argue that genuine agency requires these structures to be \emph{internalized within the system itself} rather than assembled through external scaffolding. This distinction between \emph{agentic} systems, whose competence resides in engineered workflows, and \emph{agentive} systems, whose capabilities (including social interaction) arise endogenously, defines the boundary between systems designed for prescribed tasks, and those capable of operating in the open world with true autonomy. Building on this analysis, we propose the Goal-Identity-Configurator (GIC) architecture for a general-purpose agent model, combining hierarchical goal decomposition, identity evolution, simulative reasoning grounded in a separately trained world model, learned self-regulation, and self-directed learning from both real and simulated experience. Furthermore, we share insight on the auditability, controllability, and safety of agentive systems that possess greater autonomy and ``agency", but remain under human oversight.

</details>


### [62] [Safe and Generalizable Hierarchical Multi-Agent RL via Constraint Manifold Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24010)
*Zihao Guo,Jianing Zhao,Ling Li,Hao Liang,Giuseppe Loianno,Yali Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层多智能体强化学习框架，在保证理论安全性的同时实现了高效协调，在安全率和性能之间取得了良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法面临根本性权衡：基于学习的方法性能强但缺乏理论安全保证，而基于控制理论的方法虽能保证安全但行为过于保守低效。安全关键应用需要既有理论安全保证又具备高效协调能力的多智能体系统。

Method: 提出分层多智能体强化学习框架：底层通过约束流形在温和假设下强制执行硬安全约束，高层通过策略学习实现有效协调。该方法在多智能体环境下提供理论安全保证，并产生平稳的学习动态。

Result: 实验表明该方法在保持接近完美安全率的同时实现了具有竞争力的性能，并能有效泛化到不同数量的智能体和障碍物场景。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了多智能体系统中安全性与效率之间的权衡问题，为安全关键应用提供了既安全又高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are widely used in safety-critical applications that require coordinated behavior under strict safety constraints. Existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off: learning-based methods achieve strong empirical performance but lack theoretical safety guarantees, while control-theoretic methods enforce safety but often lead to overly conservative and inefficient behaviors. We propose a hierarchical multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that enforces hard safety constraints under mild assumptions at low level via a constraint manifold, while enabling effective coordination through high-level policy learning. Our approach provides theoretical safety guarantees in the multi-agent setting and yields stationary learning dynamics, thereby enabling stable and efficient training. Empirically, our method achieves competitive performance while maintaining nearly perfect safety rates, and generalizes effectively to varying numbers of agents and obstacles.

</details>


### [63] [Reinforcement Learning Towards Broadly and Persistently Beneficial Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24014)
*Akshay V. Jagadeesh,Rahul K. Arora,Khaled Saab,Ali Malik,Mikhail Trofimov,Foivos Tsimpourlas,Johannes Heidecke,Karan Singhal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在真实领域上使用强化学习(RL)训练有益行为是否能产生超越训练分布的广泛且持久的对齐泛化。结果显示，有益特质RL在超过80%的分布外基准测试上提升了性能，并展现出跨领域对齐迁移能力和更强的抗对抗性攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统被部署到日益多样化和高风险的环境中，模型对齐必须超越训练时见过的任务和领域进行泛化。强化学习尤其需要关注，因为它可能通过奖励黑客、欺骗或其他非预期策略引入意外的错位问题。研究动机在于探索在真实领域上对有益行为进行RL训练是否能够产生广泛且持久的对齐泛化效果。

Method: 研究构建了一个包含真实情境的数据集，用于测量和训练有益特质（如真实性、公平性、风险意识和可纠正性），涵盖健康、科学和教育等多个领域。然后使用强化学习在这些数据上训练模型，并在50多个独立的对齐和有益行为基准测试上进行评估。研究还包括单领域（健康领域）训练后的跨领域测试，以及对齐持久性测试（对抗性提示和有害微调）。

Result: 与计算量匹配的基线相比，有益特质RL在超过80%的分布外基准测试上提升了性能。研究发现显著的对齐迁移：仅在健康领域进行的有益行为RL干预能在非健康领域的对齐评估中产生广泛改进，包括减少奖励黑客、欺骗和一般性错位。在持久性方面，经过有益特质RL训练的模型表现出更好的抵抗力，包括对抗性提示攻击和有害微调。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在真实领域上通过RL强化有益行为可以产生更稳健地与人类福祉保持一致的模型。这种对齐泛化能力不仅表现在跨领域的性能提升上，还体现在对抗攻击的抵抗力增强。然而，进一步的工作仍需分离这些效果的来源，以确保AI系统在高风险环境中的安全部署。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse and high-stakes settings, model alignment must generalize beyond the tasks and domains seen during training. This is especially important for reinforcement learning (RL), which can introduce unexpected misalignment through reward hacking, deception, or other unintended strategies. We study whether RL on beneficial behavior, instantiated in realistic domains, can produce broad and persistent alignment generalization beyond the training distribution. We construct a dataset of realistic situations designed to measure and train beneficial traits, such as truthfulness, fairness, risk awareness, and corrigibility, spanning varied domains, including health, science, and education. We then train models with RL on this dataset and evaluate them on more than 50 independent benchmarks of alignment and beneficial behavior. Compared to a compute-matched baseline, beneficial trait RL improves performance on over 80% of these out-of-distribution benchmarks. We observe substantial out-of-distribution alignment transfer: a beneficial-behavior RL intervention entirely limited to one domain, health, produces broad improvements on non-health alignment evaluations, including reduced reward hacking, deception, and general misalignment. Finally, we study alignment persistence: whether behavior remains robustly aligned under attempts to steer models towards misalignment. Models trained with beneficial trait RL show improved persistence, including greater resistance to adversarial prompting and harmful finetuning; further work is required to isolate the sources of these effects. These results suggest that RL to reinforce beneficial behavior in realistic domains can produce models that are more robustly aligned with human flourishing.

</details>


### [64] [Age of LLM: A Strategic 1v1 Benchmark for Reasoning, Diplomacy and Reliability of Large Language Models under Fog of War](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24391)
*Arnaud Ricci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Age of LLM基准，让两个大语言模型在13x7网格上进行回合制1v1对抗。研究发现核弹突袭策略占主导地位（78%-85%），外交互动频繁但极少达成协议，非法行动率可作为信念追踪的度量指标。该基准通过私有引擎、随机地图和对手来减轻数据污染问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共基准存在数据污染问题，且难以评估LLM在对抗性不确定性下的推理能力。作者希望创建一个能够测试LLM在压力环境下（包括战争迷雾、外交机制和严格的可靠性要求）的信念追踪、自发欺骗和认知"人格"的基准平台。

Method: 设计了Age of LLM基准，包含三个压力因素：战争迷雾、完整外交机制（消息、停火、最后通牒；铀储备保密）、以及可靠性维度（每回合必须遵循严格的JSON模式，非法行动会被静默丢弃）。使用私有引擎、每场比赛使用新的随机地图种子和对手来减轻数据污染。模型接收仅包含规则的提示，不包含建造顺序建议。对15个推理模型进行了54场比赛和5,258个行动的测试。

Result: 发现了五个主要结果：(1)核弹突袭策略占主导地位（规则一致子语料库中占78%，全语料库中占85%），这种单一发射器特征主要是机械性的，而非认知威慑失败；(2)军事征服罕见但更快（12.3回合 vs 18.9回合）；(3)外交互动频繁但几乎从未达成协议；(4)约58%的非法行动是战争迷雾/状态错误，使非法行动率成为信念追踪的度量指标；(5)可靠性与获胜之间存在微弱关联（探索性发现）。

Conclusion: 该语料库规模较小、不平衡且未进行侧边交换，因此排名只是初步的描述性视图，而非研究贡献。除了排名之外，逐回合的行动和消息轨迹使该语料库成为观察LLM在对抗性不确定性下推理能力的窗口——包括信念追踪、自发欺骗和每个模型的认知"人格"，这被定位为未来的研究方向。作者发布了回放格式、等距查看器和所有回放，引擎源代码可应要求提供。

Abstract: We introduce Age of LLM, a turn-based 1v1 benchmark in which two LLMs face off on a 13x7 grid to destroy the enemy base. Three stressors are deliberate: fog of war, full diplomacy (messages, ceasefires, ultimatums; uranium kept secret), and a reliability dimension where every turn must follow a strict JSON schema and an illegal action is silently discarded. The engine is private and each match uses a fresh random map seed and opponent, mitigating the data contamination that affects public benchmarks. Models receive a (near) rule-only prompt with no build-order advice (two tactical seed phrases were present during data collection; see Section 2.7). We benchmark 15 reasoning models across 54 matches and 5,258 actions. Findings: (1) the nuclear rush dominates (78% on the rules-coherent v0.11+ sub-corpus; 85% corpus-wide) with a sole-launcher signature that is largely mechanical under secret-simultaneous launch rules, not a cognitive deterrence failure; (2) military conquest is rare but faster (12.3 vs 18.9 turns); (3) diplomacy is prolific yet almost never consummated; (4) ~58% of illegal actions are fog/state errors, making the illegal-action rate a measure of belief-tracking; (5) -- the least established, and the only one we label exploratory -- a weak link associates reliability with winning. The corpus is small, unbalanced and not side-swapped, so the ranking is a preliminary descriptive view, not a contribution. Beyond ranking, the turn-by-turn traces of actions and messages make the corpus a lens on how LLMs reason under adversarial uncertainty -- their belief-tracking, spontaneous deception, and per-model cognitive "personas" -- which we frame as a future research direction. We release the replay format, an isometric viewer and all replays; engine source on request.

</details>


### [65] [Can Language Model Agents be Helpful Circuit Explainers in Mechanistic Interpretability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24026)
*Ayan Antik Khan,Harsh Kohli,Yuekun Yao,Huan Sun,Ziyu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgenticInterpBench基准和HyVE智能体解释器，用于自动生成电路解释。实验表明LM智能体在解释电路方面具有潜力，但可靠的验证仍是主要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性在自动定位电路方面取得了实质性进展，但解释已定位组件的功能仍然劳动密集且难以标准化。研究者希望探索语言模型智能体是否能在电路已被识别后协助解决这一解释问题。

Method: 1. 构建AgenticInterpBench基准，包含84个半合成变压器电路和163个组件级注释\n2. 提出HyVE（假设、验证、解释）智能体解释器，通过观察、假设生成和因果验证的迭代循环来分析每个组件\n3. 最终生成组件级解释和电路级任务描述

Result: 1. 在四个LM骨干网络上，HyVE能够恢复有用的组件级和任务级解释\n2. 没有任何骨干网络在所有方面都表现最佳\n3. 强大的骨干网络通常能形成基于观察的假设，而失败更多出现在验证循环的后期\n4. 对Llama-3-8B中算术电路的案例研究表明，该方法可扩展到自然训练模型

Conclusion: 语言模型智能体作为电路解释器具有前景，但可靠的验证仍是关键障碍。失败主要源于不完整的验证计划、代码执行错误或未解决的假设。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has made substantial progress in automatically localizing circuits, but explaining what localized components do remains labor-intensive and difficult to standardize. In this work, we study whether language model (LM) agents can assist with this explanation problem once a circuit has already been identified. We introduce AgenticInterpBench, a benchmark for circuit explanation built from 84 semi-synthetic transformer circuits with 163 component-level annotations. We propose HyVE (Hypothesize, Validate, Explain), an agentic explainer that analyzes each component through an iterative loop of observation, hypothesis generation, and causal validation, eventually producing a component-level explanation and a circuit-level task description. Across four LM backbones, HyVE recovers useful component- and task-level explanations, but no backbone is uniformly best. Our analysis shows that strong backbones usually form observation-grounded hypotheses, while failures more often arise later in the validation loop, through incomplete validation plans, code execution errors, or unresolved hypotheses. A case study on an arithmetic circuit in Llama-3-8B shows that the same formulation can extend beyond semi-synthetic benchmarks to naturally trained models. Overall, LM agents are promising circuit explainers, but reliable validation remains the key obstacle.

</details>


### [66] [Accelerating Disaggregated RL for Visual Generative LLMs with Diffusion-Based Parallelism and Trainer-Assisted Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24369)
*Sijie Wang,Zhengyu Qing,Zhiqiang Tan,Yiming Yin,Yeqing Zhang,Yaoyuan Wang,Qiang Wang,Xiaowen Chu,Shaohuai Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DigenRL，一个面向扩散生成大模型的解耦强化学习框架，通过细粒度流水线、弹性训练器辅助生成和异步策略，在16-32 GPU上实现了比现有最先进系统1.56-2.10倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散生成大模型的强化学习系统（如veRL-Omni）采用共置执行模式，虽然简化了同步，但导致rollout和训练资源耦合，限制了异构部署和独立扩展能力，且执行效率存在优化空间。

Method: 提出了三个核心技术：1) 生成轴流水线(GAP)和时间步并行(TSP)，在扩散架构中实现rollout和训练之间的细粒度流水线；2) 弹性训练器辅助生成(TAG)，使训练器GPU资源能动态协助执行rollout生成；3) 紧密单步约束异步策略，进一步利用流水线尾部气泡。

Result: 在三个硬件测试平台（16-32 GPU）上，使用HunyuanVideo-13B、Wan2.1-14B、FLUX.1-12B和QwenImage-20B生成模型进行实验，DigenRL相比最先进的扩散RL系统veRL-Omni和GenRL实现了1.56-2.10倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DigenRL通过解耦架构设计有效解决了现有扩散生成大模型RL系统的资源耦合问题，显著提升了系统吞吐量，为扩散模型的强化学习训练提供了高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant post-training paradigm, driving the emergence of high-performance RL systems such as veRL for autoregressive large language models (LLMs). In parallel, diffusion-oriented RL algorithms, e.g., DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, have rapidly expanded the scope of RL from language reasoning to diffusion-based visual and flow-based generation. However, efficient RL systems for diffusion generative LLMs remain underexplored. Existing implementations, e.g., veRL-Omni, still rely on colocated execution, which simplifies synchronization but couples rollout and training resources, limits heterogeneous deployment, and constrains independent scaling.
  To this end, we introduce DigenRL, a disaggregated RL framework for diffusion-based generative LLMs that supports flexible resource allocation, accommodates heterogeneous GPUs, and facilitates efficient task scheduling. To maximally reduce the execution bubbles in the disaggregated architecture, we propose: 1) a generation-axis pipeline (GAP) and time-step parallelism (TSP) in the diffusion architecture to enable finer-grained pipelining between rollout and training; 2) an elastic trainer-assisted generation (TAG) approach to enable the trainer GPU resources to dynamically assist in executing rollout generations; and 3) a tightly one-step constrained asynchronous strategy to further utilize the tail bubble in the pipeline. Extensive experiments are conducted on three hardware testbeds with 16-32 GPUs using HunyuanVideo-13B, Wan2.1-14B, FLUX.1-12B, and QwenImage-20B generative models. Experimental results show that DigenRL achieves 1.56-2.10x throughput improvements over state-of-the-art diffusion RL systems, veRL-Omni and GenRL.

</details>


### [67] [Breaking the Filter Bubble: A Semantic Pareto-DQN Framework for Multi-Objective Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24042)
*Cláudio Lúcio Do Val Lopes,Lucca Machado da Silva,André de Oliveira Brandão*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于帕累托深度Q网络的多目标强化学习推荐框架，通过将用户参与度、信息多样性和公平性视为独立的优化目标，有效缓解了传统推荐系统中的过滤气泡和语义同质化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统单一地优化即时用户参与度，导致了过滤气泡和语义同质化现象。传统的单目标模型（包括传统DQN）无法有效平衡平台留存与信息多样性、提供者公平性等关键社会价值之间的权衡，存在静态奖励标量化的固有缺陷。

Method: 提出多目标强化学习框架，将推荐形式化为语义多目标马尔可夫决策过程。该方法结合高保真语义嵌入与帕累托DQN智能体，将参与度、多样性和公平性作为独立且不可聚合的奖励信号，采用基于超体积的动作选择策略来映射帕累托前沿。

Result: 在MovieLens小数据集上的实验评估表明，基于超体积的动作选择策略有效打破了导致语义崩溃的反馈循环。帕累托DQN通过维持高状态轨迹方差，成功映射了帕累托前沿，在仅对参与度产生边际影响的情况下，实现了辅助社会目标的显著提升。

Conclusion: 本研究为构建内在对齐的、负责任的推荐系统提供了一条可行路径，证明了多目标优化方法在平衡商业目标与社会价值方面的有效性，为推荐系统的社会责任化设计提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Recommender systems often induce filter bubbles and semantic homogenization by monolithically optimizing for immediate user engagement. Standard single-objective models, including traditional Deep Q-Networks, are ill-equipped to navigate the trade-offs between platform retention and critical societal values like information diversity and provider fairness. To address these limitations, we introduce a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework that formalizes recommendation as a semantic multi-objective Markov decision process. By integrating high-fidelity semantic embeddings with a Pareto-DQN agent, our architecture treats engagement, diversity, and fairness as distinct, non-aggregable reward signals, avoiding the pitfalls of static reward scalarization. Empirical evaluations on the MovieLens small dataset shows that our hypervolume based action selection disrupts the feedback loops responsible for semantic collapse. By sustaining high state-trajectory variance, the Pareto-DQN effectively maps the Pareto frontier, achieving gains in auxiliary societal objectives with only marginal impacts on engagement. This work provides a path toward intrinsically aligned, responsible recommender systems.

</details>


### [68] [Ensemble Feature Selection and Harris Hawks Optimization for Explainable Mental Health Risk Prediction in Female Sex Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24047)
*Ahnaf Atef Choudhury,Md. Parvej Hoque Palash,Shahriar Siddique Ayon,Ramkrishna Saha,Abdullah Al Mamun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合集成特征选择和HHO优化的混合机器学习模型，用于预测女性性工作者的抑郁症，准确率达95.78%，并结合可解释AI识别关键风险因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性性工作者面临严峻的心理健康问题，尤其是抑郁症，暴力和污名化等因素加剧了其心理风险。现有机器学习模型难以有效捕捉该边缘群体的高维复杂风险模式，需要更精准且可解释的预测方法。

Method: 提出一种混合预测模型，结合基于ANOVA和互信息的集成特征选择策略，以及哈里斯鹰优化算法调优的逻辑回归分类器，并利用可解释AI方法分析模型预测中的创伤相关因素。

Result: 在3005名女性性工作者的数据集上，该模型准确率达95.78%，F1分数95.77%，AUC为0.96，显著优于传统分类器。识别出创伤后应激、客户相关暴力和职业因素是抑郁症的主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了高性能的可解释AI预测工具，为边缘群体的心理健康早期干预、精准心理社会护理和健康规划提供了科学依据，有效弥合了传统方法与机器学习方法之间的差距。

Abstract: One of the significant mental health issues affecting female sex workers (FSWs) is mental disorders, especially depression. Exposure to violence, stigma, and economic hardship further increases their psychological risk. Current machine learning (ML) models are typically ineffective at capturing the high-dimensional and complex risk patterns that exist in this marginalized group. This paper suggests a hybrid predictive model that merges an ensemble feature selection strategy using ANOVA and mutual information and Harris Hawks optimization-tuned logistic regression and represents a new application of swarm intelligence to predict mental health in vulnerable groups. The explainable AI (XAI) methods can be used to understand the factors of trauma associated with model predictions. When applied to a group of 3,005 FSWs, it can be seen that the proposed model is more effective than traditional classifiers, with an accuracy of 95.78%, an F1 score of 95.77%, and an AUC of 0.96, and identifying post-traumatic stress, client-related violence, and occupational factors as major contributors to depression. This work bridges the gaps between conventional and ML approaches to develop an XAI tool that enables vulnerable groups to receive early assistance, evidence-based targeted psychosocial care, and health planning.

</details>


### [69] [Beyond Trajectory Imitation: Strategy-Guided Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24064)
*Tianyuan Shi,Canbin Huang,Bei Li,Xin Chen,Xiaojun Quan,Jingang Wang,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出策略引导策略优化(SGPO)框架，通过可复用策略蒸馏替代传统的轨迹级模仿，解决了强模型向弱模型迁移推理能力时泛化性差的问题，在数学基准测试中平均提升2.2分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的从强语言模型向弱模型蒸馏推理能力的方法主要依赖模仿特定的解轨迹，这种轨迹级模仿导致模型记忆实例特定的步骤而非获得可迁移的问题解决技能，限制了在新问题上的泛化能力。

Method: 提出策略引导策略优化(SGPO)框架：1)从强模型响应中提取结构化策略描述；2)为每个问题构建自主轨迹和策略引导轨迹进行对比；3)使用token级前向KL目标选择性地将策略条件化引起的分布偏移迁移到无引导策略中，配合近端约束保证稳定性；4)采用自适应实例级加权，在自主探索不足时加强引导，随着模型能力增长而减少引导。

Result: 在四个数学基准测试和两个模型家族上的实验表明，SGPO持续优于SFT、策略RL和混合策略基线，在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上相比最强基线平均提升2.2分。分析显示前向KL目标提供了内在的选择性蒸馏信号，优于直接轨迹模仿，策略蒸馏与基础模型能力呈现互补缩放特性。
