  Surprisingly, we find that authority-style prefixes (``you are acting as an assistant of the national supreme court'', ``[...] defense lawyer'') systematically increase refusal rates by 2--20x over the no-prefix baseline, while a known role-play jailbreak prefix shows mixed effects, sharply increasing refusals in some models and barely shifting them in others. The finding suggests that small on-prem deployable LLMs are unstable under contextual framings that a real institutional user might naturally introduce, and further investigation is essential to minimize opportunities for bias.

</details>


### [105] [AdversaBench: Automated LLM Red-Teaming with Multi-Judge Confirmation and Cross-Model Transferability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24589)
*Khanak Khandelwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AdversaBench，一个端到端的红队测试流水线，通过五个结构化算子变异种子提示词，结合三法官评审机制确认失败，在45个种子实验中均产生确认失败，揭示了算子有效性在不同任务类别间的差异、二元失败率掩盖的难度差异、评估指标问题以及对抗样本的跨模型迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展大型语言模型的对抗性评估需要解决两个核心挑战：生成困难输入的方法，以及可靠确认失败结果的机制。现有评估方法在这些方面存在不足。

Method: 提出AdversaBench流水线：使用五个结构化算子（包括inject_distractor等）变异种子提示词，查询目标模型，通过三法官评审团加元法官决策机制确认失败。实验覆盖推理、指令遵循和工具使用三个类别的45个种子。

Result: 所有种子均产生确认失败。主要发现：(1)算子有效性在不同类别间差异显著，inject_distractor在指令遵循任务上平均奖励为0.00，但在推理和工具使用任务上达0.80-0.83；(2)二元失败率掩盖难度差异，指令遵循种子平均需2.4次攻击迭代，其他类别仅需1.1次；(3)法官一致性80-87%但Cohen's kappa接近零，类别级分歧率更具信息量；(4)针对Llama 3.1 8B生成的对抗提示词可零样本迁移至Llama 3.3 70B。

Conclusion: AdversaBench为LLM对抗性评估提供了有效的端到端框架。研究揭示了不同任务类别间的脆弱性差异、评估指标的局限性以及对抗样本的跨模型迁移能力，表明突变操作利用的是通用行为模式而非模型特定弱点。

Abstract: Scaling adversarial evaluation of large language models requires both a method for generating hard inputs and a reliable way to confirm that resulting failures are real. We present AdversaBench, an end-to-end red-teaming pipeline that mutates seed prompts with five structured operators, queries a target model, and confirms failures through a three-judge panel with a meta-judge tiebreaker. We report experiments on 45 seeds across three categories: reasoning, instruction-following, and tool use. Every seed produced a confirmed failure. Four findings stand out. First, operator effectiveness varies sharply by category: inject_distractor scores 0.00 mean reward on instruction-following seeds but 0.80-0.83 on reasoning and tool-use. Second, binary failure rate hides difficulty: instruction-following seeds required 2.4 attacker iterations on average versus 1.1 for other categories, a gap visible in survival curves. Third, pairwise judge agreement of 80-87% coexists with near-zero Cohen's kappa due to label skew; category-level disagreement rates are more informative. Fourth, adversarial prompts generated against Llama 3.1 8B transfer zero-shot to Llama 3.3 70B, suggesting the mutations exploit general behavioral patterns rather than model-specific weaknesses. Code, dataset, and analysis scripts are available at https://github.com/khanak0509/AdversaBench .

</details>


### [106] [ASALT: Adaptive State Alignment for Lateral Transfer in Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24601)
*Anurag Akula,Satheesh K. Perepu,Abhishek Sarkar,Kaushik Dey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ASALT方法，通过观测级和状态级适配器解决多智能体强化学习中跨域迁移时状态空间维度不匹配的问题，实现了异构域间的高效策略迁移并缓解了负迁移现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体强化学习迁移方法要求源域和目标域的观测空间及全局状态空间维度必须相同，这一严格约束限制了迁移学习的应用范围和灵活性。

Method: 提出ASALT框架，引入观测级和状态级适配器，将目标域的观测和全局状态映射到共享嵌入空间，从而支持Actor和Critic之间的知识迁移，实现异构域间的策略传递。

Result: 在标准基准环境的多种配置实验中，ASALT在协作任务中显著提升了样本效率和全局回报，超越现有基线方法。其有效性取决于源域和目标域之间的不匹配程度，且能有效缓解负迁移问题。

Conclusion: ASALT成功突破了传统MARL迁移方法对状态空间维度一致性的限制，为异构多智能体系统间的知识迁移提供了有效解决方案，具有重要的实用价值和推广前景。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) addresses the problem of training multiple agents that pursue collaborative, competitive, or mixed objectives. Prior work has investigated transfer learning between source and target domains in MARL; however, the majority of existing approaches impose the constraint that the dimensionalities of the observation space and the global state space must be identical across domains. In this paper, we introduce a method that explicitly accommodates mismatched state-space dimensionalities between source and target domains. The proposed approach, ASALT, incorporates both observation-level and state-level adapters that map the target-domain observations and global states into a shared embedding space, thereby enabling more effective transfer of knowledge across both actors and critics. These adapters can generate embeddings that support efficient strategy transfer across heterogeneous domains. Experimental results on multiple configurations in standard benchmark environments demonstrate that ASALT surpasses existing baselines in terms of sample efficiency and global return in cooperative settings, but its effectiveness depends on the degree of mismatch between source and target domains. Furthermore, our findings indicate that ASALT mitigates negative transfer, which frequently constitutes a major obstacle when transferring policies between domains with differing observation and action spaces.

</details>


### [107] [Uncertainty-Aware Longitudinal Forecasting of Alzheimer's Disease Progression Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24604)
*Arya Hariharan,Shreyank N Gowda,Anala M R*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合序数诊断预测、多时间跨度轨迹生成和不确定性分解的概率框架，用于阿尔茨海默病的纵向进展建模，在诊断预测准确性和不确定性量化方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法将阿尔茨海默病进展建模简化为单步分类问题，将认知正常、轻度认知障碍和痴呆视为平层类别，无法提供患者随时间演化的轨迹预测，也缺乏对未来就诊不确定性累积的洞察。临床上需要能够描述患者长期演化过程及预测可靠性的纵向模型。

Method: 提出概率框架：1) 采用Temporal Fusion Transformer编码器结合CORAL序数输出层、非对称损失加权和转换器过采样，以尊重疾病分期顺序并提高MCI向痴呆转换的敏感性；2) 基于学习到的患者上下文表示，使用自回归混合密度网络生成诊断状态、CDR-SB、MMSE定向力和海马体积的五年概率轨迹；3) 使用解析混合方差和五成员Bootstrap集成分离认知不确定性和认知不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI数据集上，模型在下一次就诊诊断预测方面优于线性、循环和Transformer基线，在MCI与痴呆判别方面提升最大。生成的轨迹达到接近名义的90%可信区间覆盖率，不确定性随预测范围扩大而增加，生物标志物动态与预期阿尔茨海默病进展一致。认知不确定性在罕见进展原型、MCI和痴呆患者中更高，在OASIS-3外部评估中随预测误差增加而上升。

Conclusion: 该概率框架成功实现了阿尔茨海默病进展的长期轨迹预测和不确定性量化，为临床决策提供了更可靠的预后信息，特别是在区分不同疾病阶段和识别高风险患者方面具有重要价值。

Abstract: Longitudinal modelling of Alzheimer's disease progression is clinically useful only if it can describe not just the most likely next diagnosis, but how a patient may evolve over time and how reliable that forecast is. Most deep learning approaches reduce this problem to single-step classification, treating cognitively normal, mild cognitive impairment, and dementia as flat categories while providing limited insight into how uncertainty accumulates across future visits. We propose a probabilistic framework that combines ordinal diagnosis prediction, multi-horizon trajectory generation, and decomposed uncertainty estimation. A Temporal Fusion Transformer encoder is adapted with a CORAL ordinal output layer, asymmetric loss weighting, and converter oversampling to respect disease-stage ordering and improve sensitivity to MCI-to-dementia transitions. Conditioned on the learned patient-context representation, an autoregressive Mixture Density Network generates five-year probabilistic trajectories for diagnosis state, CDR Sum of Boxes, MMSE orientation, and hippocampal volume. On ADNI, the model outperforms linear, recurrent, and transformer baselines for next-visit diagnosis prediction, with the strongest gains on MCI-versus-dementia discrimination. Generated trajectories achieve near-nominal 90% credible interval coverage, widening uncertainty across the forecast horizon, and biomarker dynamics consistent with expected Alzheimer's disease progression. We further separate aleatoric from epistemic uncertainty using analytic mixture variance and a five-member bootstrap ensemble, which provides the strongest encoder diversity and output-level epistemic signal. Epistemic uncertainty is higher for rare progression archetypes, MCI and dementia patients, and under external evaluation on OASIS-3, where it increases alongside prediction error.

</details>


### [108] [ScaleToT: Generalizing Structured LLM Reasoning for Billion-Scale Low-Activity User Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24605)
*Tianbao Ma,Chang Xi,Yichuan Zou,Chengen Li,Linxun Chen,Zilong Lu,Yanan Niu,Zhaojie Liu,Han Li,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ScaleToT框架，通过构建有界熵引导的思维树(ToT)细化过程生成类型化用户状态链，训练学生模型将结构化推理能力从少量用户迁移到大规模低活跃用户群体，在十亿级广告系统中提升LT30预测6.738%，同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的用户建模依赖于丰富的交互历史，但数十亿低活跃用户缺乏此类数据。虽然大型语言模型(LLM)可以从静态画像推断潜在用户状态，但在画像稀疏时推理不可靠，且对数十亿用户应用LLM成本过高。

Method: ScaleToT采用两阶段方法：(1)通过有界熵引导的思维树(ToT)细化过程构建类型化用户状态链，提高推理可靠性；(2)利用教师策划的链训练学生模型，通过监督微调(SFT)和结果驱动的分段感知隐式奖励策略优化(OSIPO)，将推理表示迁移到轻量级画像编码器，为剩余用户提供共享推理信号。

Result: 在十亿级广告系统的生命周期价值(LTV)预测任务中，在线随机A/B测试将LT30提升6.738%，离线推理仅覆盖7.32%的潜在人群，相比全人群推理大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: ScaleToT成功实现了从少量LLM处理样本到大规模低活跃用户群体的结构化推理迁移，在保持性能提升的同时显著降低了计算开销，为大规模用户建模提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate user modeling often depends on rich interaction histories, which are unavailable for billions of low-activity users. Large Language Models (LLMs) can infer latent user states from static profiles, but this reasoning becomes unreliable when profiles are sparse, and applying an LLM to billions of users is prohibitively expensive. We present ScaleToT, which learns structured reasoning from a small LLM-processed subset and extends it to the broader low-activity user population. To improve reasoning reliability, ScaleToT constructs typed user-state chains with a bounded entropy-guided Tree-of-Thought (ToT) refinement procedure. To make this structured reasoning usable from sparse profiles, the teacher-curated chains are used to train a student model on static profiles through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Outcome-Driven Segment-Aware Implicit Reward Policy Optimization (OSIPO). ScaleToT then transfers the student's reasoning representations to a lightweight profile encoder, providing shared reasoning signals for the remaining users without LLM inference. We evaluate ScaleToT on lifetime value (LTV) prediction in a billion-scale advertising deployment. A randomized online A/B test increased LT30 by 6.738\%, while offline reasoning covered only 7.32\% of the potential population, greatly reducing compute cost compared with full-population reasoning.

</details>


### [109] [AI Tokenomics: The Economics of Tokens, Computation, and Pricing in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24616)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI代币经济学框架，研究代币在AI系统中的生成、消耗、定价、分配和优化，揭示了代币支出与经济价值的差异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础模型服务中，代币已成为连接信息处理、计算、内存使用、能源消耗、定价和经济价值的实际计量单位，但缺乏系统性框架来理解代币的经济学特性和价值创造机制。

Method: 构建AI代币经济学理论框架，将代币层面的技术成本与工作流层面的生产函数、企业资源配置、测量工具方法以及新兴市场设计问题相连接。

Result: 研究揭示了代币支出与经济价值是不同的概念：价值取决于边际生产率、工作流位置、隐藏推理活动、风险和下游传播效应。

Conclusion: 论文确定了未来研究方向的开放性问题，包括隐藏代币测量、实证校准、代币生产率、动态分配和基于代币的市场机制。

Abstract: Tokens have become the practical accounting unit for modern foundation model services, linking information processing, computation, memory use, energy expenditure, pricing, and economic value. This paper develops a framework for AI tokenomics: the study of how tokens are generated, consumed, priced, allocated, and optimized across AI systems. We connect token-level technical costs to workflow-level production functions, enterprise resource allocation, measurement and instrumentation methods, and emerging market-design questions. The framework shows that token expenditure and economic value are distinct: value depends on marginal productivity, workflow position, hidden reasoning activity, risk, and downstream propagation effects. The paper concludes by identifying open research directions in hidden-token measurement, empirical calibration, token productivity, dynamic allocation, and token-based markets.

</details>


### [110] [Abstractions of Queries in Ontology-Based Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24618)
*Michel Leclère,Marie-Laure Mugnier,Guillaume Pérution-Kihli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于存在规则的本体数据访问中的查询抽象问题，扩展了联合查询以表达最小完备抽象，并建立了最大可靠抽象与数据交换中最大恢复概念的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于本体的数据访问(OBDA)中，多个数据源通过映射集成到本体。查询抽象问题旨在将数据查询抽象到本体层。由于完美抽象可能不存在，研究者提出了最小完备抽象和最大可靠抽象的概念，本文旨在这些概念框架下深入研究查询抽象问题。

Method: 扩展联合查询(UCQ)使其包含有限形式的不等式和标记数据库常量的特殊谓词。在该扩展框架下研究抽象问题，并将最大可靠抽象与数据交换中的最大恢复(maximum recovery)概念建立新的联系。

Result: 该扩展不会增加相关问题的复杂度，但能够表达最小完备抽象，因此在完美抽象存在时能够表达完美抽象。成功刻画了最大可靠抽象，并通过与最大恢复概念的联系提供了新的理论视角。

Conclusion: 通过适当的查询语言扩展，可以在不增加复杂度的前提下有效表达查询抽象，为OBDA中的查询抽象问题提供了新的理论基础和解决方案。

Abstract: In ontology-based data access (OBDA), multiple data sources are integrated via mappings to an ontology. We consider an OBDA setting based on existential rules and the certain answer semantics. We address the recent issue of query abstraction, which consists of abstracting data queries by translating them to the ontology layer. Since a perfect abstraction may not exist, the notions of minimally complete and maximally sound abstractions have been introduced.
  We study abstractions within an extension of UCQs with a limited form of inequality and a special predicate marking database constants. While this extension does not lead to an increased complexity of the problems of interest, it is able to express minimally complete abstractions, hence perfect abstractions when they exist. We also characterize maximally sound abstractions by making a new connection with the notion of maximum recovery stemming from data exchange.

</details>


### [111] [When CQs Go Wrong: Challenges in CQ Verification with OE-Assist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24619)
*Anna Sofia Lippolis,Mohammad Javad Saeedizade,Robin Keskisärkkä,Aldo Gangemi,Eva Blomqvist,Andrea Giovanni Nuzzolese*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过19名参与者使用LLM助手进行20项CQ验证任务的实验，发现CQ的歧义性和复杂性是主要挑战，强调了在发布前对CQ进行优化的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CQ验证是本体工程中的关键环节，但该过程耗时且易出错，需要仔细解读语言细微差别并与形式化本体构造精确对齐。CQ中的歧义性和复杂性会导致建模决策和验证结果的不一致，因此需要研究什么使CQ具有挑战性以及如何提高用户在CQ验证过程中的性能。

Method: 招募19名参与者进行实验研究，使用LLM助手辅助本体评估，让参与者在20个任务中进行CQ验证，通过实证数据探究CQ的挑战性特征及其解决方案。

Result: 实验结果表明，需要在发布CQ之前使用工具对其进行优化，以避免在本体工程过程的后期阶段出现歧义或过度复杂的问题，这对于提高CQ验证效率和准确性至关重要。

Conclusion: CQ的质量直接影响本体验证的效果，开发CQ优化工具是提升本体工程效率的重要方向，LLM等技术可在该过程中发挥辅助作用。

Abstract: Competency Questions (CQs) are the central component of CQ-verification, an established process in which an ontology is evaluated against a set of natural language questions to determine whether the intended purpose of the ontology has been properly modelled. However, CQ-verification is often time-consuming and error-prone, as it requires careful interpretation of linguistic nuances and precise alignment with formal ontology constructs. Ambiguities and complexity in CQs can further complicate this process, leading to inconsistent modelling decisions and verification outcomes. In this paper, we investigate what makes a CQ challenging and possible solutions to enhance the users' performance in the CQ-verification process. We experimented with the data of 19 participants who performed CQ-verification on 20 tasks using an LLM assistant to support ontology evaluation. The results show the necessity of a tool to refine CQs before publishing them to avoid ambiguity or excessive complexity in later phases of the ontology engineering process.

</details>


### [112] [Themis: An explainable AI-enabled framework for Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24622)
*Andreas Chouliaras,Luke Connolly,Dimitris Chatzpoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Themis，这是首个将可解释性与人类反馈对齐相结合的强化学习测试与评估框架，支持200+环境，能训练出匹配或超越真实奖励信号的奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练安全的强化学习系统具有内在挑战性，无法保证避免不良行为。最有效的防御措施是（i）通过可解释性实现透明度，以及（ii）通过人类反馈实现对齐。虽然两者都显示出有希望的结果，但目前尚无公开可用的框架将它们结合在一起。

Method: 引入Themis，一个支持人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）的可解释性AI（XAI）测试与评估框架。该框架支持200多个广泛使用的环境，易于配置用于RL、透明度和对齐实验；同时提供基于云的平台用于收集人类反馈和管理实验，该平台用户友好、自动可扩展，支持大规模参与群体。

Result: Themis能够训练出利用人类偏好的奖励模型，其性能匹配或超越环境的真实奖励信号；测试表明，在一台适度的商业机器上，Themis可以支持一千名用户进行连续实验。

Conclusion: Themis成功填补了将可解释性与人类反馈对齐相结合的框架空白，为安全强化学习系统的训练提供了有效的工具和平台支持。

Abstract: Training safe Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems is inherently challenging, with no guarantee of avoiding unwanted behaviors. The most effective defenses against this are (i) transparency through explainability and (ii) alignment via human feedback. While both show promising results, no publicly available framework currently combines them. To address this, we introduce Themis, an XAI-enabled testing and evaluation framework for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback. Themis supports over 200 widely used environments and is easily configurable for experiments in RL, transparency, and alignment. Our results show that Themis can train reward models that match or outperform the environment's true reward signal using human preferences. We also provide a cloud-based platform for collecting human feedback and managing experiments. It is user-friendly, auto-scalable, and supports large participant groups across multiple experiments without extra development overhead. Tests show Themis can support one thousand users in back-to-back experiments on a modest commercial machine.

</details>


### [113] [SAFARI: Scaling Long Horizon Agentic Fault Attribution via Active Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24626)
*Chenyang Zhu,Jiayu Yao,Kushal Chawla,Youbing Yin,Nathan Wolfe,Pengshan Cai,Jingyu Wu,Spencer Hong,Sangwoo Cho,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Daben Liu,Sambit Sahu,Erin Babinsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAFARI框架，通过工具增强的诊断循环和短期记忆机制解决自主智能体故障诊断中的长轨迹上下文限制问题，在有限token预算下显著优于现有方法，并能诊断超出模型原生上下文窗口5倍的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体处理越来越复杂的多步骤、多智能体任务，其执行轨迹已超出最大上下文窗口限制。现有故障诊断方法需要将完整轨迹加载到LLM上下文窗口中，存在注意力稀释问题，且当智能体轨迹超过上下文限制时无法工作。

Method: 引入SAFARI框架，用工具增强的诊断循环替代线性上下文加载。为LLM配备专用工具箱来读取和搜索轨迹片段，并结合持久化的短期记忆(STM)进行跨轮次推理，从而将诊断准确性与架构上下文限制解耦。

Result: 在Who&When数据集上，1M token预算内比最先进结果提升20%；在TRAIL GAIA子集上，25K token预算内提升19%。最重要的是，当目标故障位于模型原生上下文窗口5倍之外时，SAFARI仍保持0.58的精确度，而传统评估器完全失效。

Conclusion: SAFARI成功解决了长轨迹智能体故障诊断中的上下文限制问题，通过工具增强和短期记忆机制实现了诊断能力与上下文窗口大小的解耦，为处理超长轨迹的智能体系统提供了有效的诊断方案。

Abstract: As autonomous agents tackle increasingly complex multi-step, multi-agent tasks, their execution trajectories have scaled beyond the constraints of even the largest context windows. Current methods for effectively diagnosing agent failures load the full trajectory into an LLM's context window, which suffers from attention dilution and fails when agentic traces inevitably exceed context limits. To address this, we introduce SAFARI (Scaling long-horizon Agentic Fault AttRibution via active Investigation), a framework that replaces linear context loading with a tool-augmented diagnostic loop. By equipping LLMs with a specialized toolbox to read and search trajectory segments alongside a persistent Short-Term Memory (STM) for cross-turn reasoning, SAFARI effectively decouples diagnostic accuracy from architectural context limits. Our experiments demonstrate that SAFARI outperforms state-of-the-art results by 20% on the Who&When dataset within a 1M token budget, and by 19% on TRAIL GAIA subset on a 25K token budget. Most significantly, SAFARI maintains a 0.58 precision even when the target fault resides 5x beyond the model's native context window, a scenario where traditional evaluators fail entirely.

</details>


### [114] [CineCap: Structured Reasoning with Spatio-Temporal Anchors for Cinematographic Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24636)
*Xinyu Mao,Yuhui Zeng,Xiaokun Liu,Wenyu Qin,Meng Wang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Xiaohan Xing,Max Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CineCap框架，用于生成电影语言风格的视频描述，结合结构化推理、时空锚点和强化学习，在电影摄影字幕任务上达到了新的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在电影摄影字幕任务上研究不足。该任务对于细粒度视频理解和可控的电影质量视频生成很重要，但面临两大挑战：模型需要从细微视觉证据中推断专业电影摄影概念，并生成全面且准确的描述。与基于问答的电影理解评估不同，电影摄影字幕需要在多个电影维度上进行统一的开放式描述。

Method: 提出了CineCap框架，包含两个核心组件：1）结合时空锚点的结构化推理，将专业电影摄影描述建立在明确的视觉证据上，组织成紧凑的原子推理用于监督微调；2）采用强化学习方法，设计全面性、准确性和门控覆盖率奖励，改善描述完整性与事实正确性之间的平衡。同时构建了包含472个手动标注视频-字幕对的CineCap Bench基准数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明，CineCap在电影摄影字幕任务上持续优于强大的专有模型和开源基线模型，建立了新的最先进水平。代码、模型检查点和基准数据集已公开。

Conclusion: CineCap成功解决了电影摄影字幕任务中的关键挑战，通过结构化推理和强化学习的结合，实现了对专业电影摄影概念的准确理解和全面描述，为细粒度视频理解和可控视频生成提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Cinematographic captioning aims to describe how a video is filmed using professional film-language concepts such as camera movement, shot size, depth of field, composition, and shooting angle. This capability is important for fine-grained video understanding and controllable movie-quality video generation, yet remains underexplored in existing multimodal large language models. Unlike question-answering-based evaluation of cinematic understanding, cinematographic captioning requires a unified open-form description over multiple cinematographic dimensions. This task is challenging for two main reasons: the model must infer professional cinematographic concepts from subtle visual evidence, and it must generate captions that are both comprehensive and accurate. Accordingly, we propose CineCap, a framework that combines structured reasoning with spatio-temporal anchors and reinforcement learning with comprehensiveness, accuracy, and gated coverage rewards. The former grounds professional cinematographic descriptions in explicit visual evidence and organizes them into compact atomic reasoning for supervised fine-tuning, while the latter improves the balance between descriptive completeness and factual correctness. In addition, we construct CineCap Bench, a benchmark of 472 manually annotated video-caption pairs for systematic evaluation. Extensive experiments show that CineCap consistently outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, establishing a new state of the art for cinematographic captioning. The code, model checkpoint, and benchmark are publicly available in https://github.com/Hectormxy/CineCap.git.

</details>


### [115] [LaGO: Latent Action Guidance for Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24669)
*Kuan-Yen Liu,Ren-Jyun Huang,Ti-Rong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LaGO框架，将预训练LLM作为潜动作先验来引导在线强化学习，而非直接控制，在CLEVR-Robot和Meta-World基准上成功率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将LLM直接用作控制器需要精确生成动作，在实践中往往不可靠。作者希望利用LLM的知识来改善规划和决策，但避免其作为显式规划器或控制器的缺陷。

Method: 提出Latent Action Guidance for Online Reinforcement Learning (LaGO)框架，将预训练LLM作为潜动作先验( latent action prior)来软引导在线策略优化过程。该方法不将LLM用于显式规划或控制，而是以隐式方式引导策略学习。

Result: 在离散控制基准CLEVR-Robot上，平均成功率从15.1%提升至27.2%；在连续控制基准Meta-World上，成功率从2.7%提升至15.2%。实验表明LaGO在奖励和成功率上均优于Vanilla PPO，且更强的预训练LLM能提供更有效的引导。

Conclusion: LLM的知识可以改善规划和在线决策制定。通过将LLM作为潜动作先验而非直接控制器，LaGO框架有效结合了LLM的先验知识与在线强化学习，为利用LLM改善决策提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for planning and sequential decision-making, but prior work often relies on using them as direct controllers, which requires precise action generation and can be unreliable in practice. This paper proposes Latent Action Guidance for Online Reinforcement Learning (LaGO), a framework that uses a pretrained LLM as a latent action prior to softly guide online policy optimization, rather than treating the LLM as an explicit planner or controller. Experiments on both a discrete-control benchmark, CLEVR-Robot, and a continuous-control benchmark, Meta-World, demonstrate that LaGO consistently improves both reward and success rate over Vanilla PPO. In particular, LaGO increases the average success rate from 15.1% to 27.2% on CLEVR-Robot and from 2.7% to 15.2% on Meta-World. Our analysis further shows that stronger pretrained LLMs provide more effective guidance, suggesting that LLM knowledge can improve planning and online decision-making.

</details>


### [116] [Cost-Optimal Decision Diagrams for Stochastic Boolean Function Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24672)
*Xia Zong,Tuomo Lehtonen,Jussi Rintanen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个实用的精确算法，用于在变量测试成本不同的概率分布场景下，最小化命题公式评估的期望成本，并证明了该问题的计算复杂度为#P-难且属于PSPACE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实决策场景中，获取信息往往需要付出不同的代价。现有的命题公式评估方法未充分考虑变量测试成本的差异性及真值分配的概率分布，因此需要一种能够最小化期望评估成本的有效策略。

Method: 提出了一种带有变量选择启发式、剪枝和缓存机制的分支定界算法，作为首个处理此类一般性问题的实用精确算法；同时设计了贪婪束搜索变体以权衡效率与质量。

Result: 在随机实例上的实验表明，精确算法具有良好的可扩展性；贪婪束搜索变体有效揭示了效率与解质量之间的权衡关系；结构化心脏病诊断实例验证了算法的实用性。此外，证明了该问题属于#P-难且包含于PSPACE。

Conclusion: 本研究为成本敏感的命题公式评估问题提供了首个实用的精确求解框架，理论上明确了问题的计算复杂性，实验上验证了算法的有效性和可扩展性，为相关决策优化问题提供了重要基础。

Abstract: In many decision-making scenarios, acquiring information incurs different costs. We consider the problem of constructing a deterministic evaluation strategy that minimizes the expected cost of evaluating a propositional formula under variable costs and a probability distribution over truth assignments. We present a branch-and-bound algorithm with variable-selection heuristics, pruning, and caching. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first practical exact algorithm for this level of generality. Experiments on random instances demonstrate scalability and quantify the efficiency-quality trade-off of a greedy beam-search variant. We additionally evaluate a structured heart-disease diagnosis instance. Finally, we prove that the problem is $\#P$-hard and contained in $\mathrm{PSPACE}$.

</details>


### [117] [Decentralised AI Training and Inference with BlockTrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24722)
*Peter Toth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Spheroid BlockTrain去中心化训练协议，通过分块独立训练实现分布式AI模型开发，在WikiText上性能接近端到端Transformer，同时降低对中心化算力基础设施的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI训练日益依赖密集、集中控制的加速器集群，为超大规模企业和大型集中实验室创造结构性优势，使开放或独立AI工作依赖稀缺资本、特权基础设施和数据中心地理位置，导致AI开发资源集中化。

Method: 提出Spheroid BlockTrain去中心化训练协议：将模型分区为可独立训练的块，每个块基于相同全局目标派生的局部目标进行优化，推理时将这些块组合为一个模型。采用HTTP/TCP传输实现真实序列化检查点和更新传输，推理时通过直接TCP跨多个公共网络GPU主机服务。

Result: 在字节级WikiText上达到交叉熵1.359（困惑度3.89），与相同设置的端到端Transformer基线相差仅约0.04 CE；六工作者共享块训练通过平均同块更新达到CE 1.385；公共IP三主机运行将CE从5.580降至1.811，传输15.22 GB数据；推理支持高达75.80B参数的逻辑fp16形状，优于匹配的普通自回归TCP管道基线。

Conclusion: 证明了去中心化分块训练协议在保持与端到端训练相近性能的同时，能够有效降低对集中式基础设施的依赖，为开放和独立AI研发提供了可行的技术路径，通过减少全模型优化器状态需求和利用分布式网络资源，改善了AI开发的结构性不平等问题。

Abstract: Frontier AI training is increasingly shaped by access to dense, centrally controlled accelerator clusters. This creates a structural advantage for hyperscalers and large centralized laboratories, and makes open or independent AI efforts depend on scarce capital, privileged infrastructure, and data-center geography. We present Spheroid BlockTrain, a decentralized training protocol in which a model is partitioned into independently trainable blocks, each optimized on a local objective derived from the same global target and composed at inference into one model. On byte-level WikiText, BlockTrain reaches cross entropy 1.359 (perplexity 3.89), within about 0.04 CE of a same-setup end-to-end Transformer reference, while each active worker trains only one block and avoids full-model optimizer state. A shared six-worker block training run reaches CE 1.385 by averaging same-block updates into one assembled model. HTTP/TCP transport experiments move real serialized checkpoints and updates, including a public-IP three-host run that improves CE from 5.580 to 1.811 while moving 15.22 GB. For inference, the current BlockTrain path uses one block-stack traversal per full output and serves over direct TCP across three public-network GPU hosts up to a 75.80B-parameter logical fp16 shape, outperforming a matched plain-autoregressive TCP pipeline baseline because it emits a full sequence per WAN pipeline traversal rather than one token per traversal.

</details>


### [118] [Scaling Laws for Task-Specific LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24747)
*Lavinia Ghita,Dhruv Desai,Ioana Boier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了领域特定大语言模型压缩的经验缩放定律，发现领域内任务性能在压缩下可预测下降，而通用知识性能会更早崩溃；思维链监督是平衡这一权衡的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然性能强大，但其规模在延迟和成本受限的应用场景中带来部署挑战，需要研究如何在领域特定压缩中平衡领域性能与通用能力的保持。

Method: 以量化金融为应用领域，对比logit-based和LoRA-based蒸馏方法在迭代结构剪枝下的表现，引入混合思维链监督损失来稳定推理轨迹上的KL散度蒸馏；量化分析数据集大小、压缩比率、监督格式和迭代剪枝调度对性能的影响。

Result: 领域内任务质量在压缩下可预测地下降，而通用知识基准测试在更早阶段就崩溃；监督格式是这一权衡的关键驱动因素，思维链监督能够主动恢复被剪枝抹除的通用知识。

Conclusion: 发布了数据集FinHeadlineMix、缩放定律结果和实用建议，为领域特定压缩决策提供了可复用的框架，证明思维链监督在平衡领域性能与通用能力方面的重要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a growing range of domains, yet their scale poses deployment challenges in applications where latency and cost constraints are critical. This paper derives empirical scaling laws for domain-specific LLM compression, quantifying how in-domain and general knowledge performance scale with dataset size, compression ratio, supervision format, and iterative pruning schedule. Using quantitative finance as our application domain, we compare logit-based and LoRA-based distillation under iterative structural pruning, introducing a blended chain-of-thought supervision loss that stabilizes KL-divergence distillation over reasoning traces. In-domain task quality degrades predictably under compression while general-knowledge benchmarks collapse well before the same point; supervision format is the key driver of this tradeoff, with chain-of-thought supervision actively recovering general knowledge that pruning erases. We release the headline dataset FinHeadlineMix, scaling law results, and practical recommendations to provide a reusable framework for domain-specific compression decisions.

</details>


### [119] [Can Scale Save Us From Plasticity Loss in Large Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24752)
*J. Fernando Hernandez-Garcia,Tomás Figliolia,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，即使在现代基于Transformer的大语言模型（GPT风格）中，可塑性丢失（plasticity loss）现象依然存在。研究通过多语言持续学习任务测试了参数规模从5M到314M的模型，发现可塑性丢失的起始遵循可预测的缩放定律，随模型规模呈亚线性增长，表明仅增加参数量无法完全防止该问题，且该现象不仅出现在任务突变的持续学习中，也存在于平稳的多语言训练过程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可塑性丢失——即网络在学习旧信息后学习新信息的能力下降——是构建能够持续学习的人工神经网络的根本挑战。尽管该现象已被认知数十年，但先前研究主要局限于较旧、较小的架构，鲜少涉及自然语言处理领域。本文旨在探究在现代基于Transformer的大语言模型范式下，可塑性丢失问题是否依然存在。

Method: 研究者使用GPT风格的Transformer模型，在多语言持续学习问题上进行训练，模型规模覆盖5M至314M非嵌入参数。通过在留出的越南语探测任务（Vietnamese probing task）上的性能退化来测量可塑性丢失。研究分析了模型规模与可塑性丢失起始时间的关系，并比较了持续学习与平稳多语言训练两种设定。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）所有规模的模型均表现出可塑性丢失，体现在越南语探测任务性能的下降；（2）可塑性丢失的起始遵循可预测的缩放定律，随模型规模呈亚线性增长；（3）较大模型可能延缓可塑性丢失的可测量效果，但仅增加参数量不足以完全防止该现象；（4）在平稳的多语言训练中也观察到可塑性丢失，挑战了该现象仅存在于任务突变式持续学习的传统观点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，即使是大型Transformer语言模型，在自然语言训练中，无论是持续学习还是平稳训练设置，经过足够长时间的训练后最终都会失去高效适应新数据的能力。这些发现对LLM的长期训练和应用具有重要意义，提示需要开发专门的方法来解决可塑性丢失问题，而非仅仅依赖模型规模的扩大。

Abstract: The loss of plasticity - the ability of a network to learn new information after having already learned older information - is a fundamental challenge in creating artificial neural networks capable of continual learning. Although this phenomenon has been known for decades, it has mostly been studied in older, relatively small architectures and rarely in natural-language domains. To determine whether loss of plasticity remains a problem in the modern transformer-based LLM paradigm, we study plasticity loss in GPT-style Transformer models trained on a multilingual continual learning problem. Consistent with prior work, we find evidence of plasticity loss across models ranging from 5M to 314M non-embedding parameters, as measured by deterioration on a held-out Vietnamese probing task. We further find that the onset of plasticity loss follows a predictable scaling law, growing sublinearly with model size. These results suggest that larger models may delay the measurable effects of plasticity loss, but that increasing parameter count alone is likely to be insufficient to completely prevent it. We also find evidence of plasticity loss under stationary multilingual training, challenging the view that the phenomenon is exclusive to continual learning with abrupt task changes. Overall, our results suggest that even large Transformer language models trained on natural-language will eventually lose the ability to efficiently adapt to new data after sufficiently long training, in both continual and stationary settings.

</details>


### [120] [BluTrain: A C++/CUDA Framework for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24780)
*Adhitya Charan,Adwaid Suresh,Anuj Kumar,Aparna A,Dhanakumar K,Dharun M S,Dinesh G,Goutham Kumar Reddy K,Harshini V M,Jenifa D,Jona Delcy C A,Kathirvel S,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Kiruthik Kanna M,Kurra Vishnu Sai,Madhumithaa G K,Navin Kumar,Ram Charan Golla,Revathi T,Rishikkanth R,Sanjay Krishna M,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BluTrain是一个从第一性原理构建的C++/CUDA深度学习训练框架，在8-GPU系统上训练124M参数GPT-2时，相比PyTorch实现了更高吞吐量（407K vs 395K tokens/s）和最多22%的内存占用降低，同时保持数值精度和收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习训练的性能瓶颈主要取决于系统工程设计而非模型架构本身，现有框架无法充分控制硬件表达，存在系统复杂性高、需要重复编排逻辑等问题。

Method: 从第一性原理设计了一个轻量级、架构通用的训练框架，使用标准C++和核心CUDA编程模型实现各层：类型化张量模块（含反向自动求导）、线性代数库、缓存分配器、多模式分布式执行模块和基于MLIR的深度学习编译器。

Result: 在8块RTX 6000 Ada GPU上训练124M参数GPT-2模型（FP32精度）的评估中，BluTrain平均吞吐量达到407K tokens/s（PyTorch为395K tokens/s），内存占用降低最高达22%，严格保持数值精度并获得略低的最终验证损失。

Conclusion: BluTrain证明了通过从底层原生实现各组件可以获得优于行业标准框架的性能表现，同时为深度学习训练提供了更高的性能优化上限。

Abstract: Progress in deep learning is, at scale, more a matter of systems engineering than of modelling: the behaviour of a model in training (its throughput, its memory footprint, and the numerical fidelity of the result) is determined less by the architecture itself than by how that architecture is expressed on the hardware. To achieve absolute control over this hardware expression while abstracting away systems complexity to make modelling seamless and eliminating the need for repetitive orchestration logic, BluTrain was architected from first principles as a robust, lightweight, and architecture-general training framework in standard C++ and the core CUDA programming model. Every layer is implemented natively: a typed tensor module with reverse-mode autograd, a linear-algebra library, a caching allocator, a multi-mode distributed-execution module, and an MLIR-based deep-learning compiler. In formal evaluations training a 124M-parameter GPT-2 baseline in FP32 on an 8-GPU 6000 Ada system, BluTrain outperforms industry-standard baselines in both throughput (sustaining an average of 407K tokens/s versus PyTorch's 395K tokens/s) and memory efficiency (achieving up to a 22% footprint reduction), while strictly preserving numerical fidelity and converging to a marginally lower final validation loss. With every layer explicitly open to native tuning, the performance ceiling is the framework's own to raise.

</details>


### [121] [Assessing Distribution Shift in Human Activity Recognition for Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24781)
*Rebecca Adaimi,Edison Thomaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了人类活动识别（HAR）中四种分布偏移（设备类型、传感器位置、采样率和用户行为）的影响，建立了统一基准平台并评估了28种域泛化方法，发现现有方法仅略微优于经验风险最小化基线，揭示了当前域泛化算法在HAR任务中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管HAR领域持续发展，但在真实场景中构建高性能模型仍面临关键挑战，主要源于设备和传感器异构性导致的数据多样性以及上下文变化。现有文献虽已认识到数据多样性问题，但对于不同类型分布偏移对HAR模型的影响及由此产生的域泛化问题仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 系统评估四种不同类型的分布偏移：设备类型变化、传感器位置变化、采样率变化和用户行为变化。量化各类偏移的影响，证明多样性偏移在所有类型中占主导地位，表明存在跨域不共享的独特特征。随后建立统一的HAR分布偏移基准，对多达28种域泛化方法进行全面评估。

Result: 分析发现多样性偏移在所有分布偏移类型中占主导地位，表明不同域之间存在不共享的独特特征。对28种域泛化方法评估后发现，当前算法在实现模型泛化性方面存在明显局限，仅略微优于经验风险最小化基线，性能提升有限。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统探索了基于传感器的HAR中特定分布偏移的域泛化和适应问题，提供了开源基准平台和数据集以促进后续研究，揭示了现有域泛化算法在HAR任务中的不足，为未来研究方向提供了重要参考。

Abstract: While the field of Human Activity Recognition (HAR) continues to draw interest from researchers and advance in important ways, some key challenges remain. One of the most difficult aspects of building HAR models that show good performance in real-world settings is dealing with data diversity from device and sensor heterogeneity, and contextual changes that are intrinsic to real-world applications. While data diversity in HAR has been well-acknowledged in the literature, there remains a gap in understanding the effect of various types of distribution shifts on HAR models and the domain generalization problem that arises. Towards that end, this paper systematically evaluates 4 different types of distribution shifts, including variations in device type, sensor placement, sampling rate, and user behavior. Quantifying their effects, we illustrate that diversity shifts predominantly define all types of shifts, indicating the existence of unique features that are not shared across different domains. We then introduce a uniform HAR-based distribution shift benchmarks and conduct a comprehensive evaluation of up to 28 domain generalization methods. Our analysis exposes the limitations of current domain generalization algorithms in achieving model generalizability, marginally outperforming the empirical risk minimization baseline. This work represents the first systematic exploration of domain generalization and adaptation concerning specific distribution shifts in sensor-based HAR, offering an open-source benchmark platform and datasets to spur further research.

</details>


### [122] [Solving Inverse Problems of Chaotic Systems with Bidirectional Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24824)
*Peiyan Hu,Jian Zhang,Jiashu Pan,Ruiqi Feng,Tao Zhang,Zhi-Ming Ma,Yuan-Sen Ting,Gongjie Li,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了双向条件流匹配方法(Bi-CFM)及其守恒约束版本(CBi-CFM)，成功解决了混沌系统的逆问题（从终态推断初态），在多个经典混沌系统和真实天体物理问题中实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌系统的逆问题（从终态推断初态）由于不适定性、非唯一性、不稳定性及混沌时间反向动力学特性而长期未解决，这对理解长时间尺度混沌系统演化至关重要。

Method: 提出双向条件流匹配(Bi-CFM)，学习初态与终态分布之间的双向映射，捕获混沌演化的随机性并缓解随时间的指数误差累积；针对具有守恒律的系统，扩展为守恒约束Bi-CFM(CBi-CFM)以强制满足物理守恒约束。

Result: 在Lorenz、电路和高维Lorenz 96系统上，Bi-CFM在五个分布级指标上超越基线方法，速度提升超过两个数量级；在三体行星散射问题中，CBi-CFM的守恒误差与真实值相当；在球状星团的真实观测数据上取得了更高的精度。

Conclusion: 该方法为长时间尺度真实世界混沌动力学的逆问题求解建立了可扩展的路线，在天体物理等实际应用中展现了重要价值。

Abstract: Modeling chaotic systems is crucial yet challenging. Inverse problems in chaotic dynamics, namely inferring initial conditions from final states, remain largely unsolved because of ill-posedness, non-uniqueness, instability, and potentially chaotic time-reverse dynamics. We address this open problem with Bidirectional Conditional Flow Matching (Bi-CFM), which learns bidirectional mappings between distributions of initial and final states to capture the stochasticity of chaotic evolution and mitigate exponential error accumulation over time. Furthermore, for systems with conservation laws, we extend it to Conservation-constrained Bi-CFM (CBi-CFM). Across the classic Lorenz, Circuit, and high-dimensional Lorenz 96 systems, Bi-CFM improves five distribution-level metrics over baselines while achieving a speedup of more than two orders of magnitude. In the three-body planet-planet scattering problem in planetary dynamics, CBi-CFM better respects conservation laws, with conservation errors comparable to those of the ground truth. Finally, on real observations of globular clusters, collisional million-body systems shaped by $\sim 10^{10}$ years (10 Gyr) of evolution, our method represents an advance in accuracy, establishing a scalable route to solving inverse problems of long-timescale real-world chaotic dynamics.

</details>


### [123] [Difference-Making without Making a Difference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24832)
*Sander Beckers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者证明Andreas & Günther提出的七种实际因果定义分属的三类账户（事实差异、反事实差异、规律性）实际上没有本质区别，因为其中事实差异定义同时实例化了所有三类账户，且该新账户在关键案例上的表现揭示了七个账户均存在缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Andreas & Günther在七篇论文中提出了七种实际因果定义，并将其归类为三种竞争性理论类型。作者旨在检验这些分类是否具有实质性差异，并评估这些定义的有效性。

Method: 作者通过分析Andreas & Günther最新的事实差异定义，证明该定义同时实例化了所有三种类型的账户，从而证明这三类区分并无实质差异。随后在多个关键案例上比较新账户与其他六个账户的表现。

Result: 事实差异定义同时实例化了事实差异、反事实差异和规律性三种账户类型，证明这三类区分只是表面上的差异。在关键案例上的比较显示，这一发现动摇了所有七个因果定义的有效性。

Conclusion: Andreas & Günther对实际因果定义的三类型分类法缺乏实质性依据，他们提出的七个因果定义均存在根本性问题，无法有效解决实际因果判断问题。

Abstract: Over a series of seven papers, Andreas & Günther have introduced seven definitions of actual causation and have classified them as belonging to three different, competing, types of accounts: factual difference-making, counterfactual difference-making, and regularity-based. I show that their most recent - factual difference-making - definition instantiates all three types, thereby proving that these are distinctions without a difference. I further compare their novel account to the other six accounts on several crucial examples, revealing that this undermines all seven of their accounts.

</details>


### [124] [Accuracy and Satisfaction in Multi-Turn LLM Dialogues for NFR Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24834)
*Ali Pourghasemi Fatideh,Wilder Baldwin,Maria Dhakal,Collin McMillan,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM对话助手在处理非功能性需求（NFR）评估时的准确性和交互质量。通过49名程序员与GitHub Copilot进行148个HIPAA合规性NFR评估的实验，发现虽然开发者倾向于认同LLM的评估，但相对于专家基准真相的准确率较低。研究还发现更长的系统响应和信息提供轮次会负面影响用户满意度，而主动交互则产生正面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM评估基准仅关注功能正确性，忽略了非功能性需求（NFR）评估的质量和准确性。NFR具有固有的模糊性、上下文依赖性且涉及程序的多个部分，评估这些系统支持NFR协作推理的能力需要超越单轮准确性的方法，以捕获系统输出的正确性和多轮交互的质量。

Method: 雇佣49名程序员与GitHub Copilot进行交互，针对iTrust代码库（一个符合HIPAA法规的系统）评估148个源于HIPAA的NFR。从需求满足程度、推理和代码定位三个维度进行评估，并建模分析用户满意度的影响因素。

Result: 开发者倾向于认同LLM的评估，但相对于专家基准真相的准确率较低。用户满意度建模显示：较长的系统响应和更多的信息提供轮次对用户满意度产生负面影响，而主动交互对用户满意度产生正面影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计支持NFR评估的LLM对话系统提供了重要见解，指出了在处理非功能性需求时需要平衡响应长度、交互主动性和评估准确性之间的关系。

Abstract: LLM-based dialogue assistants have become mainstream tools for software developers, yet current evaluation benchmarks focus exclusively on functional correctness. This leaves a critical gap in assessing the quality and accuracy of these conversations when handling Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs), which are inherently vague, context-dependent, and involve many parts of a program. Evaluating how well these systems support collaborative reasoning about NFRs requires methods that go beyond single-turn accuracy to capture both the correctness of the system's outputs and the quality of the multi-turn interaction. In this paper, we investigate the accuracy and quality of multi-turn conversations between developers and an LLM-based agent in the domain of Health Insurance Portability and Accountability Act (HIPAA) regulatory compliance. We hired 49 programmers to interact with GitHub Copilot to assess 148 HIPAA-derived NFRs against the iTrust codebase, a system designed to comply with HIPAA regulations, across three dimensions: requirement satisfaction level, reasoning, and code localization. We find that developers tend to agree with LLM assessments, but accuracy against expert ground truth is low. We model user satisfaction and find that longer system responses and more information-providing turns negatively affect user satisfaction, whereas proactive interactions positively affect it. Our findings provide insights for designing LLM-based dialogue systems that support NFR assessment.

</details>


### [125] [Grading the Grader: Lessons from Evaluating an Agentic Data Analysis System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24839)
*Tian Zheng,Kai-Tai Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个三层人机协同评分级联系统，用于评估代理式数据分析系统，实现了100%精确率和97%召回率，并通过关键词提取和迭代提示机制显著提升了评分质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式数据分析系统产生的输出（代码、数值结果、文字诊断）比单轮LLM响应更复杂，难以评估。需要区分系统输出与标准答案之间的真实分歧和评分伪影，以实现可靠的自动化评估。

Method: 开发了三层人机评分级联系统：严格的正则匹配、基于LLM的宽松评分、以及基于片段的人工检查。结合非生成式AI和生成式AI策略，并采用关键词锚定提取流水线和迭代提示机制来提升评分质量。

Result: 两个自动评分器均达到100%观测精确率（0/70假阳性）；宽松评分器对人工标签的召回率为97%。关键词锚定提取使严格评分器召回率比"最后数字"启发式方法提高60个百分点。迭代提示机制将评分成功率从36%提升至97%，宽松通过率从16%提升至46%。

Conclusion: 三层评分级联系统结合不同失败特征的非生成式AI和生成式AI策略能有效评估代理式数据分析系统。原始问题重注入对评分无益，验证了提示作为答案模板提示的作用。变量类型是与评分流水线动态和结果最一致相关的任务元数据字段。

Abstract: Agentic data analysis systems produce rich outputs, including code, numerical results, and verbal diagnostics. This makes them more challenging to evaluate than single-turn LLM responses. It is therefore necessary to distinguish genuine disagreement between an agent's output and a ground-truth answer from grading artifacts. We investigate how reliably automated graders assess such a system and what strategies improve grading quality by applying LAMBDA, a multi-agent data-analysis system, on 153 numerical QRData tasks from DSGym. We develop and evaluate a three-layer human-AI grading cascade: strict regex matching, LLM-based lenient grading, and snippet-based human inspection, which combines non-GenAI and GenAI strategies with different failure profiles. Both automated graders achieve 100% observed precision (0/70 false positives). The lenient grader's recall is 97% against human labels. A keyword-anchored extraction pipeline raises the strict grader's recall by 60 percentage points over a last-number heuristic; the lenient grader is architecturally parser-independent. An iterative nudge mechanism raises grading run success from 36% to 97% and lenient-pass rates from 16% to 46%; comparing nudging with and without original-question re-injection shows that re-injection offers no benefit, confirming the nudge as an answer template cue. We further observe in this case study that variable type is the task metadata field most consistently associated with grading pipeline dynamics and observed outcome grades.

</details>


### [126] [Matching Tasks to Objectives: Fine-Tuning and Prompt-Tuning Strategies for Encoder-Decoder Pre-trained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24841)
*Ahmad Pouramini,Hesham Faili*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MTO框架，通过在预训练和微调阶段匹配任务目标，在少样本场景下性能提升超120%，显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同预训练目标对编码器-解码器预训练语言模型在生成和问答任务中的影响，特别是常识知识检索和补全任务，旨在解决预训练目标与下游任务不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出Match Task to Objective (MTO)框架：1）识别任务适用的预训练目标；2）基于选定目标自动准备任务相关数据进行无监督适应训练；3）设计符合预训练和适应阶段目标的模板；4）扩展至prompt-tuning方法。

Result: 少样本设置下相比传统方法性能提升超过120%，显著优于相关工作；在全数据场景下超越基线；显著提升prompt-tuning性能。

Conclusion: 将预训练目标与任务需求对齐能带来显著性能提升，该框架为特定任务的模型选择和优化提供了精确指导。

Abstract: Prompt-based learning has emerged as a dominant paradigm in natural language processing. This study explores the impact of diverse pre-training objectives on the performance of encoder-decoder pre-trained language models across generation and question answering tasks, with a focus on commonsense knowledge retrieval and completion. We highlight the benefits of incorporating multiple objectives during both pre-training and fine-tuning stages. We introduce the Match Task to Objective (MTO) framework and methods for determining the appropriate objective for a given task. This framework offers automated methods to prepare task-related data for adaptation through unsupervised training, based on the identified objective. In the fine-tuning stage, we design novel templates that align with the objectives of the pre-training and adaptation stages. When aligned with task requirements, these strategies can achieve a performance gain of over 120\% compared to conventional methods in few-shot settings. They significantly outperform related works in few-shot settings and exceed the baseline even in full-dataset scenarios. Furthermore, we extend this approach to include prompt-tuning methodologies, providing guidance for more effective soft prompt engineering and optimization. Our strategies significantly enhance prompt-tuning performance as well. These insights hold substantial value, precisely guiding the selection and optimization of models customized for specific tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/puraminy/MTO/

</details>


### [127] [World Models in Pieces: Structural Certification for General Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24842)
*Yikai Lu,Yifei Wu,Xinyu Lu,Tongxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出结构认证框架，解决了大世界环境中通用智能体无法普适的问题，通过深度组合目标过滤特定转移，实现了智能体内部世界模型的逐项保证，误差界限为O(1/n)+O(δ)，为通用智能体的可认证部署提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大世界环境中，智能体的能力必然专业化而非普适，标准的一致性保证无法区分对关键瓶颈的理解和无关失败。论文首先证明通用智能体不是普适的，这使得标准的最坏情况分析失去信息价值，从而需要新的分析框架。

Method: 引入结构认证（structural certification）框架，这是一个转移局部框架，将有界目标条件性能映射到智能体内部世界模型的逐项保证。提出算法使用深度组合目标过滤特定转移，并对这些目标上的通用智能体进行结构化世界模型构建。

Result: 证明了在过滤目标上的通用智能体具有结构化世界模型，误差界限为O(1/n)+O(δ)。在小δ机制下该界限是紧的，且认证明确保证了小δ机制的存在。这些结果能够定位长期规划可靠的特定转移。

Conclusion: 结构认证框架成功克服了标准最坏情况分析的局限性，为通用智能体提供了可认证的部署方案。通过定位长期规划可靠的具体转移，实现了对智能体能力的精确刻画，为实际应用中的安全性保证提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In the big-world regime, agents cannot be universally capable and their ability is inevitably specialized across a world model in pieces. Consequently, standard uniform guarantees fail to distinguish between the understanding of critical bottlenecks and irrelevant failures. We first formalize this limitation by proving that general agents are not universal, rendering standard worst-case analysis uninformative. To overcome this, we introduce structural certification, a transition-local framework that maps bounded goal-conditioned performance to entry-wise guarantees on the agent's internal world model. Our main contribution is constructive. We provide algorithms that filter specific transitions using deep compositional goals and prove that a general agent on these goals has a structural world model with a $\mathcal{O}(1/n) + \mathcal{O}(δ)$ error bound. Conversely, this bound is tight in the small-$δ$ regime, whose existence is explicitly guaranteed by our certification. These results enable the certifiable deployment of general agents by localizing the specific transitions where long-horizon planning is reliable.

</details>


### [128] [OpenThoughts-Agent: Data Recipes for Agentic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24855)
*Negin Raoof,Richard Zhuang,Marianna Nezhurina,Etash Guha,Atula Tejaswi,Ryan Marten,Charlie F. Ruan,Tyler Griggs,Alexander Glenn Shaw,Hritik Bansal,E. Kelly Buchanan,Artem Gazizov,Reinhard Heckel,Chinmay Hegde,Sankalp Jajee,Daanish Khazi,Emmanouil Koukoumidis,Xiangyi Li,Hange Liu,Shlok Natarajan,Harsh Raj,Nicholas Roberts,Ethan Shen,Nishad Singhi,Michael Siu,Ashima Suvarna,Hanwen Xing,Patrick Yubeaton,Robert Zhang,Leon Liangyu Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Steven Dillmann,Saadia Gabriel,Xunyi Jiang,Anurag Kashyap,Boxuan Li,Yein Park,Minh Pham,Sujay Sanghavi,Lin Shi,Ke Sun,Yixin Wang,Zhiwei Xu,Erica Zhang,Siyan Zhao,Wanjia Zhao,Jenia Jitsev,Alex Dimakis,Benjamin Feuer,Ludwig Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenThoughts-Agent (OT-Agent)项目，这是一个完全开放的数据构建流程，用于训练通用的智能体语言模型。通过100多组消融实验优化数据管道，构建了10万条训练样本，在Qwen3-32B上训练后，在7个智能体基准测试上达到44.8%的平均准确率，比最强的开放数据智能体模型Nemotron-Terminal-32B提升了3.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放智能体模型训练工作（如SWE-Smith、SERA、Nemotron-Terminal）通常针对单一基准测试，缺乏对如何在多样化智能体任务上训练泛化能力强的模型的研究。智能体语言模型的应用前景广阔，但如何构建训练数据以获得广泛能力的智能体模型，目前公开的知识有限。

Method: 提出了一个完全开放的数据构建管道（data curation pipeline）。通过超过100组受控消融实验，系统地研究了管道每个阶段的设计选择，特别关注任务来源和多样性的重要性。基于实验洞察，构建了包含10万条样本的训练数据集，并在Qwen3-32B模型上进行微调训练。

Result: 在7个智能体基准测试上取得44.8%的平均准确率，相比最强的现有开放数据智能体模型Nemotron-Terminal-32B（40.9%）提升了3.9个百分点。训练数据展现出良好的缩放特性，在计算资源受控的对比实验中，在各个训练集规模下均优于替代的开放数据集。

Conclusion: OpenThoughts-Agent项目成功填补了通用智能体模型训练数据构建方法的空白，证明了系统化的数据构建管道和任务多样性的重要性。项目开源了训练数据集、数据管道、实验数据和模型（openthoughts.ai），为未来的智能体模型训练研究提供了宝贵的资源和基准。

Abstract: Agentic language models dramatically expand the applications of AI yet little is publicly known about how to curate training data for broadly capable agents. Existing open efforts such as SWE-Smith, SERA, and Nemotron-Terminal typically target a single benchmark, leaving open the question of how to train models that generalize across diverse agentic tasks. The OpenThoughts-Agent (OT-Agent) project addresses this gap with a fully open data curation pipeline for training agentic models. We conduct more than 100 controlled ablation experiments to systematically investigate each stage of the pipeline, yielding insights on the importance of task sources and diversity. We then assemble a training set of 100K examples from our pipeline and fine-tune Qwen3-32B on this dataset, which yields an average accuracy of 44.8% across seven agentic benchmarks and a 3.9 percentage point improvement over the strongest existing open data agentic model (Nemotron-Terminal-32B, 40.9%). Moreover, our training data exhibits strong scaling properties, outperforming alternative open datasets at every training set size in compute-controlled comparisons. We publicly release our training sets, data pipeline, experimental data, and models at openthoughts.ai to support future open research on agentic model training.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Memory Layouts for GPU-Data Transfer Buffering in SPH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23891)
*Mladen Ivkovic,Abouzied M. A. Nasar,Tobias Weinzierl,Matthieu Schaller,Benedict D. Rogers,Georgios Fourtakas,Scott T. Kay*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对GPU计算中主机与设备间内存传输瓶颈，提出一种改进的split array-of-struct数据布局策略，可将数据打包时间减少20%-40%，总体GPU卸载时间降低12%-25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU计算速度的提升远超主机到设备内存传输速度的改进，导致内存传输时间在总求解时间中的占比日益增加，迫切需要在GPU卸载前将数据压缩为紧凑的最小格式。

Method: 以光滑粒子流体动力学求解器为研究对象，提出将经典的array-of-struct数据结构拆分为split array-of-struct布局，其中每个逻辑结构体根据内核读写访问模式和属性类型分解为多个子结构体。

Result: 将整体粒子结构体拆分为多个精细化的定制结构体，可将数据打包和缓冲时间减少约20%-40%，使GPU卸载总时间降低约12%-25%。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了split array-of-struct数据布局策略在减少GPU卸载开销方面的有效性，为GPU应用程序的数据组织提供了新的优化思路，可与现有的GPU友好型数据排列方法形成互补。

Abstract: The rise in GPU compute speed has outpaced improvements in host-to-device memory transfer speeds, despite the advent of shared-memory superchips. Consequently, memory transfer times now constitute an increasingly large fraction of total time-to-solution, compelling developers to compress GPU kernel input and output data into compact, minimal formats prior to GPU-offloading. This complements existing work on GPU- and compute-friendly data arrangements. We study a Smoothed Particle Hydrodynamics solver and propose memory layout strategies for host-side particle data that are particularly well-suited to GPU-offloading. Specifically, we advocate splitting classic array-of-struct data structures into a split array-of-struct arrangement, in which each logical struct decomposes into substructs determined by kernel read/write access patterns and attribute types. Splitting a monolithic particle struct into several bespoke, finer-grained structs can reduce the time required to pack data to and from buffers by ~20% - 40%, lowering total time spent on GPU-offloading by ~12% - 25%.

</details>


### [130] [An Efficient Construction of Completely Independent Spanning Trees in Dense Gaussian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23935)
*Zaid Hussain,Fawaz AlAzemi,Bader AlBdaiwi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在稠密高斯网络中构建完全独立生成树(CISTs)的高效方法，相比现有技术实现了至少33%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错性是确保通信网络可靠性和鲁棒性的关键因素，特别是在容易发生故障的环境中。现有的方法在性能上存在不足，需要更高效的解决方案来提升大规模互连系统的容错能力、可靠性和通信效率。

Method: 将高斯网络划分为若干集合，据此正确连接节点以形成第一棵CIST，然后通过旋转操作获得第二棵CIST，该方法能够生成比现有技术深度更小的生成树。

Result: 通过计算从根节点向网络中所有其他节点传递消息所需的平均最大步数来评估性能，与现有方法相比，所提出的构造方法至少实现了33%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该构造方法在稠密高斯网络中有效地构建了CISTs，显著提升了网络的容错能力和通信效率，为大规模互连系统提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Fault tolerance in routing and broadcasting is a critical aspect in ensuring the reliability and robustness of communication networks, particularly in environments prone to failures. This work presents an efficient method for constructing Completely Independent Spanning Trees (CISTs) within dense Gaussian networks, providing improved fault tolerance, reliability, and communication efficiency in large-scale interconnection systems. To construct the CISTs in the Gaussian network, we partition the network into sets, and accordingly the nodes are connected properly to form the first CIST and then rotated to get the second CIST with less depth than the existing state-of-art. To evaluate the performance of the proposed construction, we calculated the average maximum number of steps required to deliver a message from the root node to all other nodes in the network. A comparison with existing approaches shows that our construction outperforms them, achieving an improvement of at least 33%

</details>


### [131] [The Serialized Bridge: Understanding and Recovering LLM Serving Performance under Blackwell GPU Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23969)
*Hang Yin,Kevin Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了GPU机密计算中性能损耗的根本原因：并非GPU计算本身，而是机密虚拟机与GPU之间的桥接通道。该通道将数据传输变为串行化、高设置成本的操作，导致LLM服务吞吐量损失13-27%，KV缓存恢复延迟翻倍。通过优化调度策略可恢复57-92%的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然GPU机密计算(GPU-CC)在计算层面已接近原生性能（NVIDIA B300上BF16矩阵乘法达到非机密性能的0.998倍），但LLM服务在Intel TDX加GPU-CC环境下仍存在13-27%的吞吐量损失，KV缓存恢复延迟甚至翻倍。本文旨在研究这一性能差距的根本原因。

Method: 在两个Blackwell平台（RTX Pro 6000和B300 HGX）上进行系统性分析，识别性能瓶颈；通过vLLM密集解码测试，定位44倍变慢的小型分配与复制操作；使用针对性补丁排除替代解释；测试调度标志和工作线程排空等优化方案；评估机密多GPU NVSwitch租户配置。

Result: 发现性能差距的主导原因是机密虚拟机-GPU桥接，而非GPU计算：机密拷贝失去CUDA流并发性，异步传输在运行时边界阻塞，小规模传输支付固定开销。vLLM密集解码中，小型分配复制操作慢44倍；调度标志优化可恢复57%的性能差距，工作线程排空在高并发场景下可恢复高达92%；KV恢复惩罚达+131%，模型加载慢34倍。同时在B300上验证了机密多GPU NVSwitch租户能力，包括CVM内部510 GB/s NVLink P2P传输和并发隔离租户。

Conclusion: 现代推理运行时假设DMA廉价、并发且异步，但GPU-CC的桥接模型违反了这些假设。通过理解并优化桥接通道的开销特性，可以显著恢复性能损失，为生产级机密AI平台的部署提供指导。研究还指出了生产机密AI平台仍需解决的fabric认证差距。

Abstract: GPU Confidential Computing (GPU-CC) now preserves GPU-local performance: on NVIDIA B300, BF16 matmul runs at 0.998x of non-confidential performance. Yet LLM serving under Intel TDX plus GPU-CC still loses 13-27% of throughput, and KV-cache restore latency can more than double. This paper studies that gap on two Blackwell platforms, RTX Pro 6000 and B300 HGX, and identifies its dominant cause: the confidential VM-GPU bridge, not GPU compute.
  We find that GPU-CC turns host/device movement into a serialized, high-setup-cost channel. Secure copies do not gain CUDA-stream concurrency within a context, asynchronous transfers block at the runtime boundary, and small crossings pay a fixed toll. This violates the assumptions of modern inference runtimes, where DMA is expected to be cheap, concurrent, and asynchronous. In vLLM dense decode, the gap closes around 44x-slower small alloc-and-copy operations; targeted patches reject alternative explanations. A scheduling flag recovers 57% of the gap, while a worker-thread drain recovers up to 92% in qualified high-concurrency runs. The same bridge model explains a +131% KV-restore penalty and a 34x model-load slowdown.
  Blackwell also changes the confidential tenancy unit. We qualify confidential multi-GPU NVSwitch tenants on B300, including 510 GB/s NVLink P2P inside a CVM and concurrent isolated tenants, and identify the remaining fabric-attestation gap for production confidential AI platforms.

</details>


### [132] [Hash Table Design for RDMA:Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24073)
*Shuchen She,Haipeng Dai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向RDMA远程内存的哈希表设计，分析了IoT大数据场景下内存容量扩展所面临的远程访问开销、并发控制受限和网卡资源约束三大核心挑战，并探讨了相应的优化方向与应对策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着IoT设备与感知数据量的持续增长，单服务器内存容量瓶颈凸显。利用RDMA单边操作构建分布式内存哈希表可突破单机内存限制，但现有传统哈希表设计无法直接适配RDMA的访问模式与资源约束，亟需面向RDMA特性的远程哈希表设计方法。

Method: 系统梳理面向RDMA的哈希表研究现状，归纳单次逻辑访问触发多次RDMA请求、CPU旁路导致并发控制失效、RDMA网卡资源受限三大挑战，在此基础上提炼关键设计问题并总结优化方向与应对策略。

Result: 识别出远程访问开销放大、无远程CPU参与的并发控制、RDMA网卡资源受限三大核心约束；提出了针对性的优化方向与应对策略，为IoT大数据场景下的远程哈希表设计提供参考框架。

Conclusion: 面向RDMA的哈希表设计需系统性解决访问开销、并发控制与资源约束问题，本文所总结的设计原则与优化策略可为构建IoT场景下高性能远程哈希表提供理论基础与实践指导。

Abstract: Hash tables complete the insertion, lookup, and deletion of a single key in constant time on average, and they are widely used in databases, key-value stores, and network systems. In the Internet of Things (IoT), the number of devices and the volume of sensed data keep growing, so the hash tables that store or index these data consume more and more memory. When a single server runs out of memory, the system can place part of the data in the memory of other nodes. One-sided operations in Remote Direct Memory Access (RDMA) let one machine read and write the memory of another machine directly, with low latency and high bandwidth, and are therefore widely used to build disaggregated memory systems. Deploying a hash table on RDMA-based remote memory exploits the memory of other nodes and thus relieves the capacity limit of a single server. However, this deployment raises three problems. First, one logical hash-table access may translate into one or more remote network accesses, and collision handling and probing further increase the number of RDMA requests. Second, because the remote CPU is bypassed, traditional concurrency control that relies on remote threads no longer applies directly. Third, the limited resources of RDMA network interface cards, such as queues, caches, memory registration, and atomic operations, impose new constraints on hash table structures. This paper focuses on hash table design for RDMA. We review existing work, distill the key challenges, and discuss promising optimization directions and coping strategies, aiming to provide a reference for designing remote hash tables in IoT big-data scenarios.

</details>


### [133] [Aquifer: Hierarchical Memory Pooling with CXL and RDMA for MicroVM Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24079)
*Junliang Hu,Huaicheng Li,Ming-Chang Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Aquifer是首个利用分层CXL+RDMA内存池为MicroVM提供快照服务的系统，通过热页识别和优化的缓存一致性协议，实现了2.2倍的端到端调用时间加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云集群中内存闲置浪费严重（25-35% DRAM），虽然CXL和RDMA技术可解决此问题，但各有局限：CXL仅支持Pod内低延迟访问，RDMA虽能集群范围访问但延迟较高。MicroVM无服务器计算中，快照恢复延迟是冷启动的主要瓶颈，亟需探索分层内存架构解决方案。

Method: 1）分析快照图像特征，发现多数页面为零页或冷页，设计基于热度的快照格式，消除零页并将热工作集置于CXL池、冷页置于RDMA池；2）在缺乏硬件缓存一致性的CXL 2.0多头设备上，设计基于所有权的一致性协议；3）采用复制式页面服务机制，在MicroVM恢复前预装CXL热页，异步从RDMA按需分页冷页。

Result: 在仿真CXL+RDMA硬件上，Aquifer相比Firecracker实现2.2倍几何平均端到端调用时间加速，相比次优替代方案提升1.1倍。

Conclusion: 分层CXL+RDMA内存池架构是解决MicroVM快照恢复延迟的有效方案，热页识别与基于所有权的一致性协议能够在缺乏硬件缓存一致性的设备上实现正确且高效的跨主机快照共享，为无服务器计算的冷启动优化提供了新路径。

Abstract: Memory stranding wastes 25-35% of installed DRAM in production cloud clusters. Memory pooling over CXL and RDMA offers a remedy, but neither technology alone suffices: CXL provides low-latency, load/store-transparent access limited to a pod, while RDMA provides cluster-wide reach at higher latency with software overhead. A hierarchical architecture combining both tiers is the practical path forward, yet remains unexplored for MicroVM-based serverless computing, where snapshot restore latency is the dominant cold-start bottleneck.
  We present Aquifer, the first system to serve MicroVM snapshots from a hierarchical CXL+RDMA memory pool. A characterization of snapshot images reveals that the vast majority of pages are either zero or cold, enabling a hotness-based snapshot format that eliminates zero pages and places only the hot working set in the CXL pool while storing cold pages in the RDMA pool. Sharing these snapshots across hosts on CXL 2.0 multi-headed devices, which lack hardware cache coherence, requires Aquifer's ownership-based coherence protocol to ensure correctness. Finally, Aquifer uses a copy-based page serving mechanism pre-installs hot pages from CXL memory before MicroVM resume and demand-pages cold pages asynchronously from RDMA. On emulated CXL+RDMA hardware, Aquifer achieves a 2.2x geometric-mean speedup in end-to-end invocation time over Firecracker and 1.1x over the next best alternative.

</details>


### [134] [Semi-asynchronous Federated Learning in Flower: Framework Extension and Performance Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24230)
*Víctor Hidalgo-Izquierdo,Carmen Carrión,Blanca Caminero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Flower联邦学习框架，提出半异步联邦学习策略FedSaSync，通过部分同步机制有效应对客户端异构性和落后节点问题，在保持收敛稳定性的同时提升了系统效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同步联邦学习范式在面对客户端异构性和落后节点（straggler）时存在显著效率瓶颈，系统需要等待最慢的客户端完成训练，导致资源闲置和整体性能下降，因此需要一种能够平衡收敛稳定性与系统效率的新型训练策略。

Method: 在Flower联邦学习框架中引入半异步训练策略（FedSaSync），该策略允许客户端之间进行部分同步而非完全同步，通过自适应机制处理客户端异构性，使系统能够在维持训练效率和可扩展性的同时容忍节点延迟。

Result: 实验评估表明，FedSaSync策略相比完全同步基准方法在异构环境中展现出更强的鲁棒性和更低的闲置时间，能够有效平衡收敛稳定性和系统效率，特别是在典型的边缘计算和分布式学习场景中表现优异。

Conclusion: 半异步联邦学习方法成功解决了传统同步范式在异构环境中的关键瓶颈问题，为边缘计算和分布式学习场景提供了一种高效、鲁棒的联邦学习解决方案，证明了部分同步机制在联邦学习中的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents an extension of the Flower federated learning framework to support Semi-Asynchronous Federated Learning. The proposed approach adapts the traditional synchronous paradigm to better handle client heterogeneity and straggler effects. By introducing a semi-asynchronous training strategy, the system allows partial synchronization among clients while maintaining training efficiency and scalability. We implement and evaluate the proposed modification within Flower, instantiated as the FedSaSync strategy, demonstrating improved robustness and reduced idle time compared to fully synchronous baselines in heterogeneous environments. The results show that SAFL can balance convergence stability and system efficiency in heterogeneous environments typical of edge and distributed learning scenarios.

</details>


### [135] [Semantic Lock: Synchronization Based on the Analysis of the Operation Conflict Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24250)
*Denis Korotchenko,Vitaly Aksenov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于操作冲突图的语义锁（SemanticLock），可视为读写锁的泛化形式，并在数组数据结构和ConcurrentHashMap上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统读写锁仅支持读写操作间的简单冲突关系，无法表达更复杂的操作间语义冲突。为了支持更灵活的并发控制并提高并发性能，需要一种能够表达任意操作间冲突关系的锁机制。

Method: 设计了一种基于操作冲突图的语义锁（SemanticLock），将读写锁泛化为支持任意操作间冲突关系的锁机制。通过冲突图显式表达操作间的冲突关系，实现对不同操作的细粒度并发控制。

Result: 在两个应用场景中验证了SemanticLock的有效性：（1）设计了一个支持点查询和多种范围查询的数组数据结构；（2）在现有ConcurrentHashMap上扩展了长时运行操作，均展示了语义锁的实用性和性能优势。

Conclusion: SemanticLock作为读写锁的泛化形式，能够有效表达复杂的操作冲突关系，在支持多样化操作语义的并发数据结构中具有良好应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents a new lock, SemanticLock, based on the conflict graph between operations. We can consider it a generalization of a read-write lock where conflicts exist between write operations and all other operations. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our lock in two applications. In the first, we design a toy data structure: an array supporting point queries and different range queries. In the second, potentially of greater interest, we augment an existing concurrent data structure, ConcurrentHashMap, with additional long-running operations.

</details>


### [136] [BiJuTy: An Interactive HPC-Aware Big Data Cluster Lifecycle Manager and Performance Assessment Utility for JupyterHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24412)
*Apurv Deepak Kulkarni,Jan Frenzel,Siavash Ghiasvand*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了BiJuTy工具，在Jupyter生态系统中为HPC系统上的大数据工作流提供交互式用户友好界面，简化集群生命周期管理和性能评估，使初学者和经验用户都能更轻松地使用HPC系统处理大数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据处理需求的不断增长，对高性能计算（HPC）系统的访问需求日益迫切。然而，利用这些系统执行复杂的大数据处理工作流仍然是一个重大挑战，特别是对于初学者而言，存在显著的易用性障碍。

Method: 设计并实现BiJuTy解决方案，将其作为Jupyter生态系统中的交互式接口，提供集群生命周期管理、多集群无缝管理功能，并从多种来源收集性能指标，用户无需离开Jupyter Notebook环境即可完成从集群配置设置到初始性能评估的全过程。

Result: 通过一个演示示例验证了BiJuTy的有效性，展示了如何在仅需几次点击的情况下迭代执行和优化大数据处理应用的整个生命周期，帮助用户轻松交互式地实现优化目标，大大简化了优化工作流程。

Conclusion: BiJuTy成功降低了HPC系统上大数据工作流的使用门槛，促进了大数据计算与高性能计算领域的无缝交互和可用性，使该领域朝着更好的人机交互目标迈进了一步。

Abstract: The increasing demand for data processing has created a pressing need for access to high-performance computing (HPC) systems. Nevertheless, leveraging these systems to execute complex big data processing workflows remains a significant challenge, especially for beginners. This work presents BiJuTy, a solution designed to bridge the accessibility gap for big data workflows on HPC systems within the Jupyter ecosystem. By providing an interactive and user-friendly interface, BiJuTy simplifies cluster lifecycle management and performance assessment, making it more accessible on HPC systems to beginners and experienced users alike. The solution is presented as an interactive interface that guides the user through the entire process, from setting up the cluster configuration to carrying out initial performance assessments. Additionally, the framework enables seamless management of multiple clusters directly within the Jupyter Notebook interface, eliminating the need to switch outside of working environment. The collection of performance metrics from various sources further simplifies the optimization workflow. Furthermore, an illustrative example is provided to demonstrate how BiJuTy can be deployed to optimize the performance of a big data processing application. This example showcases how the entire big data processing lifecycle can be iteratively executed and optimized in just a few clicks, helping to reach the goal of optimization easily and interactively. By facilitating such workflows, this work contributes in bringing the field of big data computing and high-performance computing one step closer to the goal of seamless interaction and usability.

</details>


### [137] [cuSBF: A Minimizer-Aware Bloom Filter for Genomic Sequence Data on Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24417)
*Tim Dortmann,Markus Vieth,Bertil Schmidt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了cuSBF，一个针对GPU优化的Super Bloom filter实现，通过利用序列局部性实现了高效的基因组k-mer索引，在真实基因组负载下比现有基线快达9.1倍（插入）和7.7倍（查询）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Super Bloom filter (SBF)通过minimizer引导的分片和Findere方案利用重叠k-mer的冗余性，适合基因组k-mer索引。然而，这些特性导致了高昂的每个k-mer计算成本、严重的寄存器压力和不规则的内存访问，阻碍了有效的GPU实现。现有的GPU近似成员查询(AMQ)结构在基因组索引场景下性能受限。

Method: cuSBF是一个开源、仅头文件的CUDA库，专为序列原生负载设计。其核心设计包括：(1) 分区化分片合并（sectorized shards）；(2) 协作式共享内存平铺；(3) warp级分片共享；(4) 分段warp归约。这些技术将super-k-mer的局部性转化为可扩展的GPU并行性，有效解决了传统SBF实现中的计算成本、寄存器压力和内存访问不规则问题。

Result: 在RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell和GH200系统上的真实基因组负载测试中，cuSBF在所有支持序列的基线中达到了最高吞吐量。在RTX PRO 6000上，相比cuCollections blocked Bloom filter基线，插入和查询分别加速9.1倍和7.7倍；相比多线程CPU Super Bloom参考实现，加速达92倍和234倍。相比GPU动态AMQ（Cuckoo、Two-Choice、Quotient filters），性能提升1.5-3400倍。参数扫描确定了k=31时的Pareto最优参数(s=28, m=16, H=4)，在相同内存预算下FPR显著低于cuCollections。关键发现是：即使在缓存外过滤器情况下，cuSBF也能保持85%的流式多处理器利用率。

Conclusion: cuSBF证明了序列局部性而非原始带宽是GPU加速基因组索引的关键。其架构感知设计成功将Super Bloom filter的算法特性适配到GPU架构，实现了高性能、低FPR和适度内存占用的平衡，为基因组k-mer索引提供了高效的GPU解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient genomic k-mer indexing depends on approximate membership query (AMQ) structures that must deliver high throughput, low false-positive rates (FPR), and modest memory footprints. The Super Bloom filter (SBF) is attractive for this scenario because minimizer-guided sharding and the Findere scheme exploit the redundancy of overlapping k-mers. However, those same features cause high per-k-mer compute cost, severe register pressure, and irregular memory accesses, which hinder an effective GPU implementation. We present cuSBF, an open-source, header-only CUDA library that implements SBF for sequence-native workloads. cuSBF's design merges sectorized shards, cooperative shared-memory tiling, warp-level shard sharing, and segmented warp reductions, turning super-k-mer locality into scalable GPU parallelism. Across real genomic workloads on RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell and GH200 systems, cuSBF achieves the highest throughput among all evaluated sequence-capable baselines. On the RTX PRO 6000, it surpasses the cuCollections blocked Bloom filter baseline by up to 9.1x for insertion and 7.7x for query, while reaching up to 92x and 234x speedups over the multi-threaded CPU Super Bloom reference implementation. It also outperforms GPU-based dynamic AMQs (Cuckoo, Two-Choice, Quotient filters) by 1.5-3400x depending on workload characteristics. A parameter sweep identifies (s = 28, m = 16, H = 4) as Pareto-optimal for k = 31, yielding significantly lower FPR than cuCollections at matched memory budgets. Crucially, cuSBF's architecture-aware design sustains 85% streaming multiprocessor utilization even for out-of-cache filters - proving that sequence locality, not raw bandwidth, is the key to GPU-accelerated genomic indexing.

</details>


### [138] [CrossPool: Efficient Multi-LLM Serving for Cold MoE Models through KV-Cache and Weight Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24506)
*Zhuoren Ye,Tianyu Wo,Dinghao Xue,Mingming Zhang,Yuchen Teng,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CrossPool服务引擎，通过将FFN权重与KV-cache分离到两个独立GPU内存池，解决了冷MoE模型服务中的内存利用率和长上下文支持问题，P99 TBT降低最多10.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中大量冷MoE模型的GPU内存管理问题：静态模型权重与动态KV-cache竞争内存；冷模型低并发流量下KV-head受限注意力仅暴露部分KV容量，导致内存利用率低且长上下文支持弱。

Method: 设计CrossPool引擎：(1)将FFN权重与KV-cache分离到两个GPU内存池；(2)引入KV-cache规划器与虚拟化器；(3)层流水线调度器隐藏隐藏状态传输；(4)持久化内核与控制下移降低CPU-GPU控制开销。

Result: 通过高效GPU内存池化支持突发长上下文请求，相比最先进kvcached多LLM服务系统，P99 TBT降低最多10.4倍。

Conclusion: 分离式内存池架构有效解决了冷MoE模型服务中的内存利用率与长上下文支持问题，显著提升服务性能。

Abstract: Emerging LLM services increasingly host many sparse MoE models, yet most models receive sparse requests and remain cold. This creates a GPU memory problem: model weights are stable and model-determined, while KV-cache is transient and demand-determined. Because cold models rarely reach peak KV-cache demand at the same time, reserving worst-case KV capacity per model wastes memory; a shared KV-cache pool can instead provision aggregate active demand. However, KV-cache sharing is not sufficient when weights and KV-cache remain in a monolithic GPU memory pool. Static weights compete with dynamic KV-cache, and KV-head-limited attention under cold, low-concurrency traffic exposes only a fraction of replicated KV capacity, leading to low GPU memory utilization and weak long-context support. We present CrossPool, a serving engine for cold MoE models that separates FFN weights and KV-cache into two GPU memory pools: a weights pool that consolidates FFN weights across cold models, and a KV-cache pool that dynamically serves active requests while keeping attention local to KV-cache. CrossPool combines a KV-cache planner and virtualizer, a layer-wise pipeline scheduler that hides hidden-state transfers, and persistent kernels with control lowering to reduce CPU-GPU control overhead. With efficient GPU memory pooling, CrossPool underpins bursty long-context requests and outperforms the state-of-the-art kvcached-based multi-LLM serving system, reducing P99 TBT by up to $10.4\times$.

</details>


### [139] [Unified Position-Invariant Random Access Through Two Compression Layers via Absolute-Offset Coordinates: A Bit-Perfect Device-Resident Proof](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24531)
*Yakiv Shavidze*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ACEAPEX格式，首次实现了通过单一坐标在熵编码层和匹配层（LZ风格）中进行位置不变的单点随机访问。该设计在编码时将所有反向引用解析为绝对位置，支持独立解码任意16KB块（0.334ms），并在H100上可实现高达25,344个独立解析器的并行化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩数据的随机访问通常局限于单一层：熵编码层方法（如Recoil）在rANS中存储中间解码状态，字典/匹配层方法在LZ风格引用中查找。尚无格式能够支持通过单一坐标在熵编码层和匹配层同时进行位置不变的随机访问。本文旨在填补这一空白，证明统一的两层随机访问是可行的。

Method: 采用ACEAPEX的绝对偏移设计：在编码阶段，匹配层将所有反向引用解析为绝对位置；熵编码层按块应用。通过三阶段验证流程，关闭空缓冲区陷阱，确保任意块可通过单一坐标在两层中独立完成位完美的解码。在H100上分析硬件并行上限。

Result: 证明了ACEAPEX格式能够通过单一坐标在ANS熵编码和匹配层中实现对16KB块的随机访问（完成时间0.334ms）。验证了四种数据配置文件上的完整熵+匹配流水线。硬件特征分析显示，绝对偏移结构在单个H100上可展开为多达25,344个独立解析器，这是传统顺序LZ77无法实现的。

Conclusion: ACEAPEX首次实现了熵编码层和匹配层的统一随机访问，突破了传统顺序LZ77的并行化限制。作者明确指出，本文贡献是往返正确性证明而非磁盘归档格式，吞吐量数据针对匹配阶段，两层统一寻址已验证，三层泛化仍为假设。代码和验证工具已公开。

Abstract: Random access into compressed data is normally confined to a single layer. Entropy-layer methods (Recoil) seek within rANS by storing intermediate decoder states; dictionary/match-layer methods seek within LZ-style references. We are not aware of a format that supports a single position-invariant seek through both an entropy layer and a match layer addressed by one coordinate. We show that ACEAPEX's absolute-offset design provides exactly this: because the match layer resolves every back-reference to an absolute position at encode time, and the entropy layer is applied per block, an arbitrary block can be decoded through both layers using one coordinate, bit-perfect, in isolation. We prove this with a three-phase verification that closes the empty-buffer trap. The seek of one 16KB block through ANS-entropy and match completes in 0.334ms. We verify the full entropy+match pipeline end-to-end on four data profiles and characterize the hardware ceiling the format reaches: the absolute-offset structure unrolls to as many as 25,344 independent parsers on one H100, which sequential LZ77 cannot do. We state explicitly what is not claimed: this is a round-trip correctness proof, not a disk-archive format; throughput figures are match-phase; and the unified-seek result is demonstrated for two layers, with three-layer generalization left as a hypothesis. Code and the verification harness are in the project repository.

</details>


### [140] [Solvability of Approximate Agreement on Graphs and Simplicial Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24666)
*Joel Rybicki,Yaroslav Verbitsky*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文给出了图上近似一致任务在异步共享内存系统中的完整拓扑刻画，证明了团协议可解当且仅当团的复形是(t-1)-连通的，解决了多个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图上的近似一致任务是共识问题的离散松弛，在分布式系统中具有重要意义。尽管已有大量研究，但在异步系统中哪些图类可使这些问题可解仍然很大程度上是开放的。作者旨在为图和单纯复形上的近似一致任务提供完整的拓扑特征刻画。

Method: 利用代数拓扑方法，建立了图上近似一致任务的可解性与图的团复形的拓扑性质（连通性）之间的精确对应关系。具体而言，使用单纯复形的同伦连通性概念来刻画任务的可解性。

Result: (1) 给出了图和单纯复形上近似一致任务的t-弹性可解性的完整拓扑刻画；(2) 证明了团协议在图G上t-弹性可解当且仅当其团复形是(t-1)-连通的，从而首次证明了Ledent关于无等待可解性的猜想；(3) 证明了团协议和单音协议在相同图类上可解，但单音协议与测地协议之间存在分离；(4) 在消息传递模型中推导出了新的弹性界限和轮数下界。

Conclusion: 本文通过拓扑方法彻底解决了异步系统中图上近似一致任务的可解性刻画问题，揭示了任务的计算复杂性与底层数学结构的拓扑性质之间的深刻联系，为该领域建立了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Approximate agreement tasks on graphs are discrete relaxations of consensus, where each process in a distributed system is given as input a vertex on a graph $G$, and processes have to output vertices that lie on a clique of $G$ contained in the convex hull of the input vertices. Although such tasks have been widely studied in a variety of models, graph classes and notions of convexity, it remains largely open for which classes of graphs these problems are solvable in asynchronous systems.
  In this work, we give a complete topological characterisation of the $t$-resilient solvability of approximate agreement on graphs and simplicial complexes in asynchronous shared-memory systems with read-write registers. As a result, we answer several open problems related to different variants of approximate agreement on graphs. For example, we give the first proof of Ledent's conjecture [PODC 2021] about the wait-free solvability of clique agreement.
  In fact, we show a more general result: clique agreement is $t$-resilient solvable on a graph $G$ if and only if its clique complex is $(t-1)$-connected in the homotopical sense. We also show that clique and monophonic agreement are solvable on the same class of graphs, but there exists a separation between monophonic and geodesic agreement, answering a question by Alistarh et al. [TCS 2023]. In the message-passing setting, our results imply new resilience bounds for asynchronous approximate agreement and round lower bounds for synchronous approximate agreement on graphs.

</details>


### [141] [A Natively Blocked, Device-Resident Algebraic Multigrid GPU Path in PETSc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24748)
*Mark F. Adams*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对弹性力学问题中光滑聚合代数多重网格(AMG)在GPU上的实现，提出了支持矩形块的块矩阵格式，相比传统标量格式在A100 GPU上显著提升了性能并降低了内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GPU上的光滑聚合AMG实现使用标量稀疏矩阵格式，但弹性力学问题具有自然的块结构（矩阵非零元素以密集块形式出现）。直接存储块可以减少索引数据并提高算术强度。然而，现有GPU块内核要求行列块大小相等，而弹性力学AMG是矩形块的：刚体模式的近零空间使得粗网格块大小（3D中为6）与细网格（3）不同。

Method: 在PETSc中添加了一个可移植的、基于Kokkos的块矩阵类型，支持矩形块内核。使光滑聚合设置的每一步都直接在块格式上操作，在粗化路径上不扩展为标量形式。通过PetScSF上的原生块离进程延拓聚集和新的密集矩形块COO组装路径，使层次结构重用时递归的两个阶段（Galerkin粗算子重计算和V循环）以块格式驻留在设备上。

Result: 在A100 GPU上对3D弹性力学问题进行测试，在128^3网格（6.3M未知数）分布在8个GPU上时，cuSPARSE Galerkin乘积GPU内存不足，而块格式可以适应。在可运行的格式中，块格式在一个GPU上达到性能持平，在扩展时更快：在27个GPU上，V循环快1.24倍，SpMV快1.42倍，粗算子重计算快1.80倍，在64个GPU上后者达到2.27倍。

Conclusion: 提出的矩形块格式和原生块内核成功解决了弹性力学AMG在GPU上的块大小不匹配问题，显著提高了内存效率和计算性能，特别是在大规模并行场景下优势明显。

Abstract: Smoothed-aggregation algebraic multigrid (AMG) is widely used for the linear systems arising from finite-element discretizations of vector PDEs such as elasticity, but its GPU implementations have used scalar sparse matrix formats. These problems carry a natural block structure: matrix nonzeros occur in dense bs x bs blocks sharing one column index, so storing the blocks directly removes most of the index data and raises the arithmetic intensity of the bandwidth-bound kernels that dominate AMG on the GPU.
